  "content": "# CrewAI Deployment Simplification Analysis Report\n\n**Date:** February 10, 2026\n**Repository:** kix-services-crewai\n**Prepared by:** Platform Engineering Analysis\n\n---\n\n## Executive Summary\n\nThis report provides an objective analysis of the CrewAI deployment architecture across the kix-services-crewai repository, with recommendations for simplification. The analysis examined:\n\n- ArgoCD deployed resources and status\n- Terraform infrastructure patterns across environments\n- Helm chart configuration complexity\n- ArgoCD application architecture\n- GCP Config Connector as a potential simplification\n\n### Key Findings\n\n| Area | Current State | Severity |\n|------|---------------|----------|\n| **Environment Parity** | Dev is architecturally different from QA/Prod | Critical |\n| **Code Duplication** | QA/Prod are 99% identical, yet maintained separately | High |\n| **Tool Overlap** | Terraform + ESO both manage secrets | Medium |\n| **App-of-Apps Pattern** | 1:1 root-to-child mapping adds unnecessary layer | Medium |\n| **Authentication Strategy** | Dev uses IAM auth, QA/Prod use password auth | Medium |\n\n### Primary Recommendation\n\n**Consolidate the deployment architecture into a single, consistent pattern across all environments.** The current state has three different approaches for what should be the same application, making promotion confidence low and maintenance burden high.\n\n---\n\n## Current Deployment Status\n\n### Cluster Resources (Dev Environment)\n\n| Component | Status | Manager | Notes |\n|-----------|--------|---------|-------|\n| cloud-sql-proxy | Running | Terraform | Standalone service |\n| crewai-web | Running | Helm/ArgoCD | 1 replica |\n| crewai-worker | Pending (ContainerCreating) | Helm/ArgoCD | Image pull issues |\n| crewai-buildkit | Running | Helm/ArgoCD | 1 replica |\n| replicated | Running | Helm/ArgoCD | License management |\n| Gateway | Programmed | Helm/ArgoCD | 34.128.129.211 |\n| ESO SecretStore | Valid | Helm/ArgoCD | ReadWrite capable |\n| ExternalSecrets | SecretSynced | ESO | 2 secrets synced |\n\n### Observed Issues\n\n1. **Worker pod stuck in ContainerCreating** - 99+ minutes, likely image pull or secret mount issue\n2. **CronJob failing** - `crewai-feature-flags-sync` job running for 110+ minutes\n3. **Mixed resource ownership** - Terraform manages proxy, Helm manages app, creates confusion\n\n---\n\n## Architecture Analysis\n\n### 1. Three Distinct Deployment Patterns\n\nThe most significant issue is that the three environments use fundamentally different architectures:\n\n| Aspect | Dev | QA | Prod |\n|--------|-----|----|----|\n| **Terraform files** | 12 files, 1,100 LOC | 5 files, 500 LOC | 5 files, 500 LOC |\n| **Helm releases** | 2 (OCI + local) | 1 (OCI only) | 1 (OCI only) |\n| **Local chart templates** | 18 files | 0 | 0 |\n| **Cloud SQL auth** | IAM (Workload Identity) | Password | Password |\n| **Cloud SQL Proxy** | Standalone K8s service | Sidecar container | Sidecar container |\n| **Secret management** | Terraform + ESO | Terraform only | Terraform only |\n| **Network policies** | Helm-managed | Not deployed | Not deployed |\n| **Gateway API** | Helm-managed | Not deployed via ArgoCD | Not deployed via ArgoCD |\n| **Database permissions job** | PreSync hook | Manual/missing | Manual/missing |\n| **K8s resource management** | Inline Terraform | gke-config module | gke-config module |\n\n**Impact:** Dev cannot reliably validate what will happen in QA/Prod because it exercises completely different code paths.\n\n### 2. Terraform Duplication Analysis\n\n```\nTotal Terraform Lines of Code: 2,141 lines across 22 files\n\nDev:  12 files, ~1,100 LOC (modular, IAM auth, Cloud SQL Proxy)\nQA:    5 files, ~500 LOC  (consolidated, password auth)\nProd:  5 files, ~500 LOC  (99% identical to QA)\n```\n\n**Key differences:**\n- Dev uses inline K8s resources; QA/Prod use `gke-config` module\n- Dev creates Cloud SQL Proxy deployment; QA/Prod rely on Helm sidecar\n- Dev uses IAM authentication; QA/Prod use password authentication\n- Dev has gateway-certificate.tf; QA/Prod do not\n\n### 3. Secret Management Complexity\n\nCurrent state has overlapping secret paths:\n\n```\nTerraform creates:\n├── GCP Secret Manager secrets (all environments)\n├── K8s Secret \"crewai-secrets\" (direct creation)\n└── Random passwords for DB, Rails, HMAC keys\n\nESO manages (Dev only):\n├── ExternalSecret → reads from Secret Manager\n└── Creates K8s Secret (duplicates Terraform's work)\n\nResult: Two paths to same data, potential conflicts\n```\n\n### 4. ArgoCD Architecture Issues\n\n| Issue | Impact | Environments |\n|-------|--------|--------------|\n| App-of-Apps with single child | Unnecessary sync layer | All |\n| Namespace inconsistency (argocd vs crewai-root) | Potential monitoring gaps | QA/Prod |\n| Hardcoded cluster IP | Fragile, breaks on cluster changes | Dev |\n| OCI URL format inconsistency | Potential sync issues | All |\n| Missing ignoreDifferences | Drift on Autopilot resources | QA/Prod |\n| Plaintext secrets in values.yaml | Security risk | Dev |\n\n---\n\n## GCP Config Connector Evaluation\n\n### Recommendation: Do NOT Adopt Config Connector\n\n**Rationale:**\n\n| Criterion | Score (1-10) |\n|-----------|--------------|\n| Resource coverage | 5 (no HMAC key support) |\n| Migration effort | 3 (4-6 weeks, high risk) |\n| Operational simplicity | 4 (adds control plane) |\n| Team expertise | 4 (Terraform is known) |\n| Value delivered | 4 (minimal improvement) |\n\n**Critical gaps:**\n- No CRD for `google_storage_hmac_key` - Terraform still required\n- Cross-project IAM complexity not simplified\n- Would create split state between Terraform and K8s API\n- ESO already provides GitOps-friendly secret management\n\n**Alternative:** Invest in Terraform drift detection and architecture consolidation instead.\n\n---\n\n## Simplification Recommendations\n\n### Priority 1: Unify Environment Architecture (Critical)\n\n**Problem:** Dev is architecturally different from QA/Prod.\n\n**Solution Options:**\n\n| Option | Effort | Risk | Recommended |\n|--------|--------|------|-------------|\n| A: Port dev local chart to QA/Prod | Medium | Low | Yes |\n| B: Remove dev local chart, use Terraform for Gateway | High | Medium | No |\n| C: Upstream resources to CrewAI chart | High | Low | Long-term |\n\n**Recommended approach (Option A):**\n1. Add the local `crewai-gateway` chart source to QA/Prod ArgoCD Applications\n2. Create `environments/qa/helm/crewai/` and `environments/prod/helm/crewai/` directories\n3. Copy templates that are environment-agnostic (Gateway, NetworkPolicy, HealthCheckPolicy)\n4. Adjust values for environment-specific settings\n\n**Outcome:** All three environments deploy two Helm releases, same architecture.\n\n### Priority 2: Consolidate QA and Prod Terraform (High)\n\n**Problem:** QA and Prod are 99% identical but maintained as separate copies.\n\n**Solution:**\n```\nenvironments/\n├── _shared/\n│   └── terraform/\n│       ├── main.tf          # Common resources\n│       ├── variables.tf     # With env-specific defaults\n│       └── modules.tf       # Module calls\n├── qa/\n│   └── terraform/\n│       ├── backend.tf       # QA-specific backend\n│       └── terraform.tfvars # QA variable values\n└── prod/\n    └── terraform/\n        ├── backend.tf       # Prod-specific backend\n        └── terraform.tfvars # Prod variable values\n```\n\n**Outcome:** 50% code reduction, single source of truth for infrastructure logic.\n\n### Priority 3: Standardize Cloud SQL Authentication (Medium)\n\n**Problem:** Dev uses IAM auth + standalone proxy; QA/Prod use password + sidecar.\n\n**Recommendation:** Standardize on **sidecar pattern with password auth** for all environments.\n\n**Rationale:**\n- Sidecar is the recommended pattern for Cloud SQL in GKE\n- Password auth is simpler operationally (no IAM user setup, no db-permissions job)\n- Dev's IAM pattern adds significant complexity (~331 LOC in database.tf)\n- Security benefit of IAM is minimal when proxy runs in same namespace\n\n**Migration steps:**\n1. Update dev Helm values to enable Cloud SQL sidecar\n2. Remove Terraform Cloud SQL Proxy deployment\n3. Remove database.tf IAM-specific resources\n4. Verify connectivity\n\n### Priority 4: Eliminate App-of-Apps Layer (Medium)\n\n**Problem:** Each root application manages exactly one child application.\n\n**Solution:** Use ApplicationSet or direct Application management.\n\n**Option A: ApplicationSet**\n```yaml\napiVersion: argoproj.io/v1alpha1\nkind: ApplicationSet\nmetadata:\n  name: crewai-environments\nspec:\n  generators:\n    - list:\n        elements:\n          - env: dev\n            cluster: https://34.78.246.181\n            targetRevision: develop\n          - env: qa\n            cluster: https://kubernetes.default.svc\n            targetRevision: main\n          - env: prod\n            cluster: https://kubernetes.default.svc\n            targetRevision: main\n  template:\n    metadata:\n      name: crewai-{{env}}\n    spec:\n      sources:\n        - repoURL: oci://registry.crewai.com/crewai/stable\n          chart: crewai-platform\n          targetRevision: 0.3.13\n          helm:\n            valueFiles:\n              - $values/environments/{{env}}/helm/values.yaml\n        - repoURL: https://github.com/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai\n          targetRevision: '{{targetRevision}}'\n          ref: values\n      destination:\n        server: '{{cluster}}'\n        namespace: crewai-{{env}}\n```\n\n**Outcome:** Single declarative resource manages all environments.\n\n### Priority 5: Remove Secrets from Git (High Security)\n\n**Problem:** Dev values.yaml contains plaintext OAuth credentials.\n\n**Location:** `environments/dev/helm/crewai/values.yaml` lines 379-382\n\n**Solution:**\n1. Create Secret Manager secret for WorkOS credentials\n2. Add to ESO ExternalSecret configuration\n3. Remove from values.yaml, use `valueFrom` pattern\n\n### Priority 6: Add Drift Detection (Low Effort, High Value)\n\n**Solution:** GitHub Actions workflow for daily Terraform plan:\n\n```yaml\nname: Terraform Drift Detection\non:\n  schedule:\n    - cron: '0 6 * * *'\n  workflow_dispatch:\n\njobs:\n  detect-drift:\n    strategy:\n      matrix:\n        environment: [dev, qa, prod]\n    runs-on: ubuntu-latest\n    steps:\n      - uses: hashicorp/setup-terraform@v3\n      - run: |\n          cd environments/${{ matrix.environment }}/terraform\n          terraform init\n          terraform plan -detailed-exitcode\n```\n\n---\n\n## Implementation Roadmap\n\n### Phase 1: Quick Wins (1-2 weeks)\n\n| Task | Effort | Impact |\n|------|--------|--------|\n| Remove hardcoded cluster IP from dev | 1 hour | Reliability |\n| Normalize OCI URL format | 1 hour | Consistency |\n| Move WorkOS secrets to Secret Manager | 2 hours | Security |\n| Add missing ignoreDifferences to QA/Prod | 1 hour | Stability |\n| Add drift detection workflow | 2 hours | Visibility |\n\n### Phase 2: Architecture Alignment (2-4 weeks)\n\n| Task | Effort | Impact |\n|------|--------|--------|\n| Port local chart to QA/Prod | 1 week | Environment parity |\n| Consolidate QA/Prod Terraform | 1 week | Maintainability |\n| Standardize on sidecar SQL pattern | 3 days | Simplicity |\n| Document unified architecture | 2 days | Knowledge |\n\n### Phase 3: Structural Improvements (4-6 weeks)\n\n| Task | Effort | Impact |\n|------|--------|--------|\n| Migrate to ApplicationSet | 1 week | Operational efficiency |\n| Consolidate secret management (Terraform OR ESO, not both) | 2 weeks | Clarity |\n| Create shared Terraform modules | 2 weeks | Reusability |\n| Align dev Terraform with QA/Prod patterns | 1 week | Consistency |\n\n---\n\n## Decision Summary\n\n| Decision Point | Recommendation |\n|----------------|----------------|\n| Adopt Config Connector? | **No** - gaps in HMAC support, adds complexity |\n| Standardize SQL auth? | **Yes** - use sidecar + password everywhere |\n| Keep app-of-apps? | **No** - migrate to ApplicationSet |\n| Consolidate Terraform? | **Yes** - shared module + tfvars pattern |\n| Unify environments? | **Yes** - critical for promotion confidence |\n| Keep ESO? | **Yes** - but remove dual Terraform+ESO path |\n\n---\n\n## Risk Assessment\n\n| Risk | Likelihood | Impact | Mitigation |\n|------|------------|--------|------------|\n| Migration causes downtime | Medium | High | Blue-green deployment, feature flags |\n| Terraform state corruption | Low | Critical | State backup before changes |\n| ArgoCD sync failures | Medium | Medium | Manual sync available, retry policies |\n| Team unfamiliar with changes | Medium | Medium | Documentation, pair programming |\n| Secrets exposed during migration | Low | High | Use sealed secrets for git commits |\n\n---\n\n## Conclusion\n\nThe current CrewAI deployment architecture has evolved organically, resulting in three different patterns across environments. The primary simplification opportunity is **architectural consolidation** - making all environments structurally identical while allowing for environment-specific configuration values.\n\n**Top 3 Actions:**\n1. Port the dev local chart to QA/Prod to achieve deployment parity\n2. Consolidate QA/Prod Terraform into a shared module\n3. Standardize on the sidecar Cloud SQL pattern\n\n**Do NOT pursue:**\n- GCP Config Connector adoption (adds complexity, doesn't solve core issues)\n- Major tooling changes (current stack is appropriate)\n\nThe goal is not to change tools but to use the existing tools consistently.\n\n---\n\n## Appendix: Resource Ownership Matrix\n\n| Resource | Created By | Managed By | Environment |\n|----------|-----------|-----------|-------------|\n| GCP Service Account | Terraform | Terraform | All |\n| GCP Secret Manager | Terraform | Terraform | All |\n| K8s Namespace | Terraform | Terraform+ArgoCD | All |\n| K8s Service Account | Terraform | Terraform | All |\n| K8s Secret (crewai-secrets) | Terraform | Terraform | All |\n| Cloud SQL Proxy (standalone) | Terraform | Terraform | Dev only |\n| Cloud SQL Proxy (sidecar) | Helm | ArgoCD | QA/Prod |\n| Gateway | Helm | ArgoCD | Dev only |\n| HTTPRoute | Helm | ArgoCD | Dev only |\n| NetworkPolicy | Helm | ArgoCD | Dev only |\n| ExternalSecret | Helm | ESO | Dev only |\n| Web/Worker Deployments | OCI Chart | ArgoCD | All |\n\n---\n\n*This report was generated through systematic analysis of the kix-services-crewai repository, live cluster inspection, and specialized architectural review.*\n"

> AGENT

## Analysis Complete

I've completed a comprehensive review of the CrewAI deployment architecture using four specialized agents analyzing Terraform, Helm, ArgoCD, and Config Connector. The full report is at [SIMPLIFICATION_ANALYSIS_REPORT.md](docs/SIMPLIFICATION_ANALYSIS_REPORT.md).

### Key Findings

**Critical Issue: Environment Architectural Divergence**

The three environments use fundamentally different patterns:

| Aspect | Dev | QA/Prod |
|--------|-----|---------|
| Terraform | 12 files, 1,100 LOC | 5 files, 500 LOC |
| Helm releases | 2 (OCI + local chart) | 1 (OCI only) |
| Cloud SQL | IAM auth + standalone proxy | Password + sidecar |
| Gateway/NetworkPolicy | Helm-managed | Not deployed |
| Secret management | Terraform + ESO | Terraform only |

This means dev cannot reliably validate what will run in production.

### Config Connector Verdict: Do NOT Adopt

Config Connector would **add complexity without solving the core issues**:
- No CRD for `google_storage_hmac_key` - Terraform still required
- Cross-project IAM complexity not simplified
- Would split state between Terraform and K8s API
- ESO already provides GitOps-friendly secret management
- Migration effort: 4-6 weeks with high risk

### Top Recommendations

1. **Unify environment architecture** - Port dev's local chart (Gateway, NetworkPolicy, ESO) to QA/Prod
2. **Consolidate QA/Prod Terraform** - They're 99% identical; use shared module + tfvars
3. **Standardize on sidecar Cloud SQL pattern** - Simpler than dev's standalone proxy + IAM
4. **Eliminate app-of-apps layer** - Each root manages exactly one child; use ApplicationSet instead
5. **Remove secrets from git** - WorkOS credentials in plaintext at [values.yaml:379-382](environments/dev/helm/crewai/values.yaml#L379-L382)
6. **Add drift detection** - Daily `terraform plan` workflow to catch configuration drift

### Current Cluster Issues Observed

- `crewai-worker` stuck in ContainerCreating (99+ minutes)
- `crewai-feature-flags-sync` CronJob failing (jobs timing out)
- Mixed resource ownership between Terraform and Helm creates confusion

### Implementation Phases

| Phase | Timeframe | Key Actions |
|-------|-----------|-------------|
| Quick Wins | 1-2 weeks | Fix hardcoded IPs, move secrets, add drift detection |
| Architecture Alignment | 2-4 weeks | Port local chart to QA/Prod, consolidate Terraform |
| Structural Improvements | 4-6 weeks | Migrate to ApplicationSet, unify secret management |

The full report includes detailed analysis of every component, decision matrices, and an implementation roadmap.

name: Deploy ArgoCD Application

on:
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      environment:
        description: 'Target environment'
        required: true
        type: choice
        options:
          - dev
          - qa
          - prod

concurrency:
  group: argocd-deploy-${{ inputs.environment }}
  cancel-in-progress: false

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  check-branch:
    name: Check Branch and Environment
    runs-on: static-ip-runner
    steps:
      - name: Validate branch-environment combination
        run: |
          BRANCH="${{ github.ref_name }}"
          ENV="${{ inputs.environment }}"

          if [[ "$BRANCH" =~ ^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$ ]]; then
            BRANCH_TYPE="tag"
          elif [[ "$BRANCH" == "main" ]]; then
            BRANCH_TYPE="main"
          elif [[ "$BRANCH" == "develop" ]]; then
            BRANCH_TYPE="develop"
          else
            echo "::error::Deployments not allowed from branch: $BRANCH"
            echo "::error::Allowed branches: main, develop, v* tags"
            exit 1
          fi

          case "$BRANCH_TYPE" in
            tag)
              if [[ "$ENV" != "prod" ]]; then
                echo "::error::Version tags (v*) can only deploy to production"
                echo "::error::Current: $BRANCH -> $ENV"
                exit 1
              fi
              ;;
            main)
              if [[ "$ENV" != "dev" && "$ENV" != "qa" ]]; then
                echo "::error::Main branch can deploy to: dev, qa (not $ENV)"
                echo "::error::For production, create a version tag"
                exit 1
              fi
              ;;
            develop)
              if [[ "$ENV" != "dev" ]]; then
                echo "::error::Develop branch can only deploy to dev environment"
                echo "::error::Current: $BRANCH -> $ENV"
                exit 1
              fi
              ;;
          esac

          echo "Branch-environment check passed: $BRANCH -> $ENV"
          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "## Access Control" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "- **Branch:** $BRANCH ($BRANCH_TYPE)" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "- **Environment:** $ENV" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "- **Status:** Authorized" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY

  deploy:
    name: Deploy to ${{ inputs.environment }}
    needs: check-branch
    uses: konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/argocd-deploy.yml@main
    with:
      application_file: environments/${{ inputs.environment }}/argocd/application.yaml
    secrets:
      argocd_auth_token: ${{ secrets.ARGOCD_AUTH_TOKEN }}

  summary:
    name: Deployment Summary
    needs: [check-branch, deploy]
    if: always()
    runs-on: static-ip-runner
    steps:
      - name: Generate Summary
        run: |
          echo "## ArgoCD Deployment Pipeline" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY

          OVERALL_STATUS="Deployed"
          if [ "${{ needs.deploy.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
            OVERALL_STATUS="Failed"
          elif [ "${{ needs.check-branch.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
            OVERALL_STATUS="Unauthorized"
          elif [ "${{ needs.deploy.result }}" == "skipped" ]; then
            OVERALL_STATUS="Skipped"
          fi

          echo "### Overall Status: $OVERALL_STATUS" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "**Environment:** ${{ inputs.environment }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "**Application:** \`${{ needs.deploy.outputs.app_name || 'N/A' }}\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "**Sync Status:** ${{ needs.deploy.outputs.sync_status || 'N/A' }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "**Branch:** ${{ github.ref_name }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "**Triggered by:** @${{ github.actor }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY

          echo "**Job Results:**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "- Branch Check: ${{ needs.check-branch.result }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "- Deploy: ${{ needs.deploy.result }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY

      - name: Check Overall Status
        if: needs.deploy.result == 'failure'
        run: |
          echo "::error::ArgoCD deployment failed. Check deploy job logs."
          exit 1
```

on:
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:

          echo "## Access Control" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY

```
name: z_ArgoCD Deploy (Reusable)

on:
  workflow_call:
    inputs:
      application_file:
        description: 'Path to ArgoCD Application YAML'
        required: true
        type: string
      argocd_version:
        description: 'ArgoCD CLI version'
        required: false
        type: string
        default: '2.13.3'
      wait_for_sync:
        description: 'Wait for sync + healthy'
        required: false
        type: boolean
        default: true
      sync_timeout:
        description: 'Sync wait timeout (seconds)'
        required: false
        type: number
        default: 300
    secrets:
      argocd_auth_token:
        required: true
    outputs:
      app_name:
        description: 'Deployed application name'
        value: ${{ jobs.deploy.outputs.app_name }}
      sync_status:
        description: 'Final sync status'
        value: ${{ jobs.deploy.outputs.sync_status }}

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  deploy:
    name: ArgoCD Deploy
    runs-on: static-ip-runner
    outputs:
      app_name: ${{ steps.extract-name.outputs.app_name }}
      sync_status: ${{ steps.result.outputs.sync_status }}

    env:
      ARGOCD_SERVER: argocd-ci.ix.konecta-digital.com
      ARGOCD_AUTH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.argocd_auth_token }}
      ARGOCD_OPTS: '--grpc-web'

    steps:
      - name: Checkout code
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Install ArgoCD CLI
        run: |
          VERSION="${{ inputs.argocd_version }}"
          ARCH="$(uname -m)"
          case "$ARCH" in
            x86_64) ARCH="amd64" ;;
            aarch64|arm64) ARCH="arm64" ;;
            *) echo "::error::Unsupported architecture: $ARCH"; exit 1 ;;
          esac

          OS="$(uname -s | tr '[:upper:]' '[:lower:]')"
          URL="https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/releases/download/v${VERSION}/argocd-${OS}-${ARCH}"

          echo "Downloading ArgoCD CLI v${VERSION} (${OS}/${ARCH})..."
          curl -sSL -o /usr/local/bin/argocd "$URL"
          chmod +x /usr/local/bin/argocd
          argocd version --client --short

      - name: Verify ArgoCD connectivity
        run: |
          argocd app list --output name > /dev/null
          echo "Authenticated to ArgoCD at $ARGOCD_SERVER"

      - name: Validate application file
        run: |
          FILE="${{ inputs.application_file }}"
          if [ ! -f "$FILE" ]; then
            echo "::error::Application file not found: $FILE"
            exit 1
          fi
          echo "Found application file: $FILE"

      - name: Extract application name
        id: extract-name
        run: |
          FILE="${{ inputs.application_file }}"
          APP_NAME=$(grep -E '^\s+name:\s' "$FILE" | head -1 | sed 's/.*name:\s*//' | tr -d '[:space:]' | tr -d '"' | tr -d "'")
          if [ -z "$APP_NAME" ]; then
            echo "::error::Could not extract application name from $FILE"
            exit 1
          fi
          echo "app_name=$APP_NAME" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
          echo "Application name: $APP_NAME"

      - name: Deploy application
        run: |
          APP_NAME="${{ steps.extract-name.outputs.app_name }}"
          FILE="${{ inputs.application_file }}"
          echo "Deploying application '$APP_NAME' from $FILE..."
          argocd app create -f "$FILE" --upsert
          echo "Application '$APP_NAME' deployed (upsert)"

      - name: Sync application
        if: inputs.wait_for_sync
        run: |
          APP_NAME="${{ steps.extract-name.outputs.app_name }}"
          echo "Syncing application '$APP_NAME'..."
          argocd app sync "$APP_NAME"

      - name: Wait for healthy
        if: inputs.wait_for_sync
        run: |
          APP_NAME="${{ steps.extract-name.outputs.app_name }}"
          TIMEOUT="${{ inputs.sync_timeout }}"
          echo "Waiting for '$APP_NAME' to be healthy (timeout: ${TIMEOUT}s)..."
          argocd app wait "$APP_NAME" --health --timeout "$TIMEOUT"

      - name: Deployment result
        id: result
        if: always()
        run: |
          APP_NAME="${{ steps.extract-name.outputs.app_name }}"
          if [ -z "$APP_NAME" ]; then
            echo "sync_status=unknown" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
            echo "::error::Could not determine application name"
            exit 1
          fi

          STATUS=$(argocd app get "$APP_NAME" -o json 2>/dev/null | grep -o '"sync":{"status":"[^"]*"' | head -1 | sed 's/.*"status":"//' | tr -d '"' || echo "unknown")
          HEALTH=$(argocd app get "$APP_NAME" -o json 2>/dev/null | grep -o '"health":{"status":"[^"]*"' | head -1 | sed 's/.*"status":"//' | tr -d '"' || echo "unknown")
          echo "sync_status=${STATUS}" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT

          echo "## ArgoCD Deployment Summary" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "**Application:** \`$APP_NAME\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "**Server:** $ARGOCD_SERVER" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "**Sync Status:** $STATUS" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "**Health Status:** $HEALTH" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "**Source:** \`${{ inputs.application_file }}\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "**Triggered by:** @${{ github.actor }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY

          if [ "$HEALTH" == "Healthy" ] && [ "$STATUS" == "Synced" ]; then
            echo "**Result:** Deployment successful" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          else
            echo "**Result:** Deployment may need attention (Health: $HEALTH, Sync: $STATUS)" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          fi
```

---
# ArgoCD Application for Kong API Gateway - Development Environment
# This manifest defines how ArgoCD should deploy Kong to the dev environment

apiVersion: argoproj.io/v1alpha1
kind: Application
metadata:
  name: kong-dev
  namespace: argocd
  labels:
    app: kong
    environment: dev
    platform: kix
    managed-by: argocd
  annotations:
    description: "Kong API Gateway for KIX Platform - Development Environment"
    documentation: "https://docs.kix-platform.konecta.com/services/kong"
  finalizers:
    - resources-finalizer.argocd.argoproj.io
spec:
  # Project assignment
  project: platform

  # Source configuration
  source:
    repoURL: https://github.com/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-kong
    targetRevision: main
    path: environments/dev/helm/kong
    helm:
      # Values files - base + DEV cluster overrides
      valueFiles:
        - values.yaml
        - values-dev.yaml
      # Release name (matches existing cluster resources)
      releaseName: kong-dev
      # Skip Helm dependency update (we'll handle this in CI/CD)
      skipCrds: false

  # Destination configuration
  destination:
    server: https://34.78.246.181
    namespace: kong

  # Sync policy
  syncPolicy:
    automated:
      # Automatically sync when Git changes are detected
      prune: true
      # Automatically correct drift
      selfHeal: true
      # Allow empty resources
      allowEmpty: false
    syncOptions:
      # Create namespace if it doesn't exist
      - CreateNamespace=true
      # Validate resources before applying
      - Validate=true
      # Use server-side apply
      - ServerSideApply=true
      # Respect ignore differences
      - RespectIgnoreDifferences=true
    retry:
      limit: 5
      backoff:
        duration: 5s
        factor: 2
        maxDuration: 3m

  # Ignore differences for certain fields
  ignoreDifferences:
    # Ignore differences in replicas (managed by HPA)
    - group: apps
      kind: Deployment
      jsonPointers:
        - /spec/replicas
    # Ignore differences in annotations added by GKE
    - group: ""
      kind: Service
      jsonPointers:
        - /metadata/annotations/cloud.google.com~1neg
        - /metadata/annotations/cloud.google.com~1neg-status

  # Health assessment
  revisionHistoryLimit: 10
```

# Kong Helm Chart Values - Development Environment
# This is a complete, self-contained chart configuration for the dev environment

# Kong dependency configuration (official Kong Helm chart)
kong:
  image:
    repository: kong
    tag: "3.5"
    pullPolicy: IfNotPresent

  # DBless mode - no database required
  env:
    database: "off"
    plugins: "bundled,prometheus"
    status_listen: "0.0.0.0:8100"
    admin_gui_url: "https://api.dev.ix.konecta-digital.com/kong-manager"
    admin_gui_path: "/kong-manager"
    admin_gui_api_url: "https://api.dev.ix.konecta-digital.com/kong-admin"

  # Kong Proxy configuration
  proxy:
    enabled: true
    type: ClusterIP
    http:
      enabled: true
      servicePort: 80
      containerPort: 8000
    https:
      enabled: false
    replicaCount: 2

  # Admin API configuration
  admin:
    enabled: true
    type: ClusterIP
    http:
      enabled: true
      servicePort: 8001
      containerPort: 8001

  # Manager UI configuration
  manager:
    enabled: true
    type: ClusterIP
    http:
      enabled: true
      servicePort: 8002
      containerPort: 8002

  # Ingress Controller configuration
  ingressController:
    enabled: true
    installCRDs: false
    replicaCount: 2
    ingressClass: kong
    env:
      feature_gates: "GatewayAlpha=true"

  # Resource requests and limits
  resources:
    requests:
      cpu: 500m
      memory: 512Mi
    limits:
      cpu: 2000m
      memory: 2Gi

  # Horizontal Pod Autoscaling
  autoscaling:
    enabled: true
    minReplicas: 2
    maxReplicas: 5
    targetCPUUtilizationPercentage: 70

  # Pod Disruption Budget
  podDisruptionBudget:
    enabled: true
    minAvailable: 1

  # GKE Autopilot compute class annotation
  deployment:
    kong:
      podAnnotations:
        cluster-autoscaler.kubernetes.io/safe-to-evict: "true"
      podLabels:
        cloud.google.com/compute-class: "Balanced"

  # Service Account configuration
  serviceAccount:
    create: true
    name: kong-sa
    annotations: {}

# Custom additions for KIX Platform

# Observability configuration
observability:
  prometheus:
    enabled: true
    port: 8100
```

---
# ArgoCD Application for Backstage - Development Environment
# This manifest defines how ArgoCD should deploy Backstage to the dev environment

apiVersion: argoproj.io/v1alpha1
kind: Application
metadata:
  name: backstage-dev
  namespace: argocd
  labels:
    app: backstage
    environment: dev
    platform: kix
    managed-by: argocd
  annotations:
    description: "Backstage Developer Portal for KIX Platform - Development Environment"
  finalizers:
    - resources-finalizer.argocd.argoproj.io
spec:
  # Project assignment
  project: platform

  # Source configuration
  source:
    repoURL: https://github.com/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-backstage
    targetRevision: main
    path: environments/dev/helm/backstage
    helm:
      valueFiles:
        - values.yaml
        - values-dev.yaml
      skipCrds: false

  # Destination: dev GKE cluster (NOT the shared/ArgoCD cluster)
  destination:
    server: https://34.78.246.181
    namespace: backstage

  # Sync policy
  syncPolicy:
    automated:
      prune: true
      selfHeal: true
      allowEmpty: false
    syncOptions:
      - CreateNamespace=true
      - Validate=true
      - RespectIgnoreDifferences=true
    retry:
      limit: 5
      backoff:
        duration: 5s
        factor: 2
        maxDuration: 3m

  # Ignore differences for certain fields
  ignoreDifferences:
    # Ignore differences in replicas (managed by HPA when enabled)
    - group: apps
      kind: Deployment
      jsonPointers:
        - /spec/replicas
    # Ignore differences in annotations added by GKE
    - group: ""
      kind: Service
      jsonPointers:
        - /metadata/annotations/cloud.google.com~1neg
        - /metadata/annotations/cloud.google.com~1neg-status
    # Ignore config checksum annotation (changes on every config update)
    - group: apps
      kind: Deployment
      jsonPointers:
        - /spec/template/metadata/annotations/checksum~1config
    # Ignore Gateway status (managed by GKE Gateway controller)
    - group: gateway.networking.k8s.io
      kind: Gateway
      jsonPointers:
        - /status
        - /metadata/annotations
    # Ignore HTTPRoute status (managed by Gateway controller)
    - group: gateway.networking.k8s.io
      kind: HTTPRoute
      jsonPointers:
        - /status
    # Ignore GCPBackendPolicy status
    - group: networking.gke.io
      kind: GCPBackendPolicy
      jsonPointers:
        - /status
    # Ignore HealthCheckPolicy status
    - group: networking.gke.io
      kind: HealthCheckPolicy
      jsonPointers:
        - /status

  # Health assessment
  revisionHistoryLimit: 10
```

# Base values for Backstage Helm chart
# This file contains default configuration that applies across all environments
# Environment-specific overrides are in values-{env}.yaml

# Image configuration
image:
  repository: ""
  tag: ""
  pullPolicy: IfNotPresent

# Replica configuration
replicaCount: 1

# Service configuration
service:
  type: ClusterIP
  port: 80
  targetPort: 7007
  annotations: {}
  labels: {}

# Service account configuration with Workload Identity
serviceAccount:
  create: true
  annotations:
    iam.gke.io/gcp-service-account: ""
  name: backstage-sa

# Database configuration (Cloud SQL with Proxy)
database:
  enabled: true
  type: postgres
  name: backstage
  user: backstage
  cloudSqlProxy:
    enabled: true
    image: gcr.io/cloud-sql-connectors/cloud-sql-proxy:2.14.0
    instanceConnectionName: ""
    resources:
      requests:
        cpu: 100m
        memory: 128Mi
      limits:
        cpu: 200m
        memory: 256Mi

# Backstage configuration
backstage:
  config: {}

# Secrets configuration
# Secrets are synced by ESO from GCP Secret Manager (ExternalSecrets created by Terraform)
secrets:
  backendSecret:
    name: backstage-backend-secret
    key: BACKEND_SECRET

# Resource requests and limits
resources:
  requests:
    cpu: 250m
    memory: 512Mi
  limits:
    cpu: 1000m
    memory: 1Gi

# Horizontal Pod Autoscaling (disabled initially)
autoscaling:
  enabled: false
  minReplicas: 2
  maxReplicas: 5
  targetCPUUtilizationPercentage: 70
  targetMemoryUtilizationPercentage: 80
  behavior:
    scaleUp:
      stabilizationWindowSeconds: 60
      policies:
        - type: Percent
          value: 100
          periodSeconds: 60
    scaleDown:
      stabilizationWindowSeconds: 300
      policies:
        - type: Percent
          value: 10
          periodSeconds: 60

# Pod Disruption Budget (disabled initially)
podDisruptionBudget:
  enabled: false
  minAvailable: 1

# Network policies configuration
networkPolicies:
  enabled: true
  allowHealthCheckIPs:
    - 35.191.0.0/16
    - 130.211.0.0/22
  allowFromSystem: true
  allowDNS: true
  allowGMPCollectors: false
  allowGoogleAPIsEgress: true
  googleAPIsCIDRs:
    - 199.36.153.8/30
    - 199.36.153.4/30
  allowInternetEgress: false
  customIngress: []
  customEgress: []

# Common labels applied to all resources
commonLabels:
  app: backstage
  platform: kix
  managed-by: helm

# Pod annotations
podAnnotations:
  cluster-autoscaler.kubernetes.io/safe-to-evict: "true"
  configmap.reloader.stakater.com/reload: backstage-config

# Pod labels (including GKE compute class)
podLabels:
  cloud.google.com/compute-class: "Balanced"

# Node selector
nodeSelector: {}

# Tolerations
tolerations:
  - key: kubernetes.io/arch
    operator: Equal
    value: amd64
    effect: NoSchedule

# Affinity
affinity: {}

# Security context (pod level)
securityContext:
  runAsNonRoot: true
  runAsUser: 1000
  runAsGroup: 1000
  fsGroup: 1000
  seccompProfile:
    type: RuntimeDefault

# Container security context
containerSecurityContext:
  runAsNonRoot: true
  runAsUser: 1000
```

```
# Backstage GCP Resources for Dev Environment
# Purpose: Minimal GCP integration - Namespace, SA, IAM, Secrets, Workload Identity
# All Kubernetes workload resources are managed by Helm chart via ArgoCD

# Provider configuration
provider "google" {
  project = var.project_id
  region  = var.region
}

# Kubernetes provider - connects to GKE cluster
provider "kubernetes" {
  host                   = "https://${data.google_container_cluster.gke.endpoint}"
  token                  = data.google_client_config.default.access_token
  cluster_ca_certificate = base64decode(data.google_container_cluster.gke.master_auth[0].cluster_ca_certificate)
}

# Data sources
data "google_client_config" "default" {}

data "google_container_cluster" "gke" {
  name     = var.gke_cluster_name
  location = var.region
  project  = var.gke_project_id
}

# Reference infrastructure data outputs for Cloud SQL instance details
data "terraform_remote_state" "infrastructure_data" {
  backend = "gcs"
  config = {
    bucket = "kd-tfstate-dev"
    prefix = "gcp/data"
  }
}

# Local variables for Cloud SQL instance details
locals {
  cloud_sql_instance_name   = data.terraform_remote_state.infrastructure_data.outputs.cloud_sql_instance_names["platform-services"]
  cloud_sql_connection_name = data.terraform_remote_state.infrastructure_data.outputs.cloud_sql_connection_names["platform-services"]
  data_project_id           = data.terraform_remote_state.infrastructure_data.outputs.data_project_id
}

# --- Namespace ---

resource "kubernetes_namespace_v1" "backstage" {
  metadata {
    name = var.backstage_namespace

    labels = {
      app         = "backstage"
      environment = var.environment
      managed-by  = "terraform"
    }
  }
}

# --- GCP Service Account ---

resource "google_service_account" "backstage" {
  account_id   = "backstage"
  display_name = "Backstage Developer Portal"
  project      = var.project_id
}

# Cloud SQL client role (connect to Cloud SQL instances)
resource "google_project_iam_member" "cloudsql_client" {
  project = var.project_id
  role    = "roles/cloudsql.client"
  member  = "serviceAccount:${google_service_account.backstage.email}"
}

# Cloud SQL instance user role (IAM database authentication)
resource "google_project_iam_member" "cloudsql_user" {
  project = var.project_id
  role    = "roles/cloudsql.instanceUser"
  member  = "serviceAccount:${google_service_account.backstage.email}"
}

# --- Kubernetes Service Account ---

resource "kubernetes_service_account_v1" "backstage" {
  metadata {
    name      = var.k8s_service_account_name
    namespace = var.backstage_namespace
    annotations = {
      "iam.gke.io/gcp-service-account" = google_service_account.backstage.email
    }
    labels = {
      app         = "backstage"
      environment = var.environment
      managed-by  = "terraform"
    }
  }

  depends_on = [kubernetes_namespace_v1.backstage]
}

# --- Workload Identity ---

resource "google_service_account_iam_binding" "backstage_workload_identity" {
  service_account_id = google_service_account.backstage.name
  role               = "roles/iam.workloadIdentityUser"

  members = [
    "serviceAccount:${var.gke_project_id}.svc.id.goog[${var.backstage_namespace}/${var.k8s_service_account_name}]"
  ]
}

# --- Backend Secret (GCP Secret Manager) ---
# TF generates the random value and stores it in GCP SM.
# ESO then syncs it to K8s via ExternalSecret.

resource "random_password" "backend_secret" {
  length  = 32
  special = false
}

resource "google_secret_manager_secret" "backend_secret" {
  secret_id = "backstage-backend-secret"
  project   = var.security_project_id

  replication {
    user_managed {
      replicas {
        location = "europe-west1"
      }
      replicas {
        location = "europe-west4"
      }
    }
  }
}

resource "google_secret_manager_secret_version" "backend_secret" {
  secret=[REDACTED].backend_secret.id
  secret_data = random_password.backend_secret.result
}

# --- Backstage Database (Service-Owned Resource) ---
# Backstage creates and owns its own database on the shared Cloud SQL instance.
# This achieves service self-containment while keeping costs low (shared instance).

resource "google_sql_database" "backstage" {
  name      = "backstage"
  instance  = local.cloud_sql_instance_name
  project   = local.data_project_id
  charset   = "UTF8"
  collation = "en_US.UTF8"
}

# --- Cloud SQL IAM User ---
# Creates the IAM database user so Backstage can authenticate via Workload Identity.
# Requires the SA to exist first and roles/cloudsql.instanceUser IAM binding above.

resource "google_sql_user" "backstage" {
  name     = trimsuffix(google_service_account.backstage.email, ".gserviceaccount.com")
  instance = local.cloud_sql_instance_name
  project  = local.data_project_id
  type     = "CLOUD_IAM_SERVICE_ACCOUNT"
}

# --- ExternalSecrets (managed by ESO, synced from GCP Secret Manager) ---

resource "kubernetes_manifest" "external_secret_iap_oauth" {
  manifest = {
    apiVersion = "external-secrets.io/v1beta1"
    kind       = "ExternalSecret"
    metadata = {
      name      = "backstage-iap-oauth"
      namespace = var.backstage_namespace
      labels = {
        app         = "backstage"
        environment = var.environment
        managed-by  = "terraform"
      }
    }
    spec = {
      refreshInterval = "1h"
      secretStoreRef = {
        kind = "ClusterSecretStore"
        name = "gcp-secret-manager"
      }
      target = {
        name           = "backstage-iap-oauth"
        creationPolicy = "Owner"
      }
      data = [
        {
          secretKey = "client_secret"
          remoteRef = {
            key = var.iap_oauth_secret_name
          }
        }
      ]
    }
  }

  depends_on = [kubernetes_namespace_v1.backstage]
}
```

```
            }
          }
        }

        # Cloud SQL Proxy sidecar
        container {
          name  = "cloud-sql-proxy"
          image = "gcr.io/cloud-sql-connectors/cloud-sql-proxy:2.14.0"
          args = [
            "--private-ip",
            "--quitquitquit",
            "--exit-zero-on-sigterm",
            local.cloud_sql_connection_name
          ]

          security_context {
            allow_privilege_escalation = false
            capabilities {
              drop = ["ALL"]
            }
            run_as_non_root = true
          }

          resources {
            requests = {
              cpu    = "100m"
              memory = "128Mi"
            }
            limits = {
              memory = "256Mi"
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }

  wait_for_completion = true

  timeouts {
    create = "5m"
    update = "5m"
  }

  depends_on = [
    kubernetes_namespace_v1.backstage,
    kubernetes_service_account_v1.backstage,
    google_sql_user.backstage,
    google_project_iam_member.cloudsql_client,
    google_project_iam_member.cloudsql_user,
    google_service_account_iam_binding.backstage_workload_identity
  ]
}

#------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Certificate Manager Resources (Service-Owned)
#------------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Backstage owns its own SSL certificate and certificate map for gateway access.
# These were previously managed centrally in kix-platform-infrastructure but
# have been migrated to service ownership for better service self-containment.

# Google-managed SSL certificate
resource "google_certificate_manager_certificate" "backstage_cert" {
  name        = "kd-ix-eur-${var.environment}-cert-backstage"
  project     = var.gke_project_id
  description = "Google-managed SSL certificate for Backstage Developer Portal"
  scope       = "DEFAULT"

  managed {
    domains = [var.gateway_hostname]
  }

  labels = {
    service     = "backstage"
    environment = var.environment
    managed-by  = "terraform"
  }
}

# Certificate map to hold the certificate
resource "google_certificate_manager_certificate_map" "backstage_certmap" {
  name        = "kd-ix-eur-${var.environment}-cert-backstage-map"
  project     = var.gke_project_id
  description = "Certificate map for Backstage Developer Portal"

  labels = {
    service     = "backstage"
    environment = var.environment
    managed-by  = "terraform"
  }
}

# Certificate map entry - maps hostname to certificate
resource "google_certificate_manager_certificate_map_entry" "backstage_certmap_entry" {
  name         = "kd-ix-eur-${var.environment}-cert-backstage-entry"
  project      = var.gke_project_id
  map          = google_certificate_manager_certificate_map.backstage_certmap.name
  certificates = [google_certificate_manager_certificate.backstage_cert.id]
  hostname     = var.gateway_hostname
}

     1→name: Deploy ArgoCD Application
     2→
     3→on:
     4→  workflow_dispatch:
     5→    inputs:
     6→      environment:
     7→        description: 'Target environment'
     8→        required: true
     9→        type: choice
    10→        options:
    11→          - dev
    12→          - qa
    13→          - prod
    14→
    15→concurrency:
    16→  group: argocd-deploy-${{ inputs.environment }}
    17→  cancel-in-progress: false
    18→
    19→permissions:
    20→  contents: read
    21→
    22→jobs:
    23→  check-branch:
    24→    name: Check Branch and Environment
    25→    runs-on: static-ip-runner
    26→    steps:
    27→      - name: Validate branch-environment combination
    28→        run: |
    29→          BRANCH="${{ github.ref_name }}"
    30→          ENV="${{ inputs.environment }}"
    31→
    32→          if [[ "$BRANCH" =~ ^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$ ]]; then
    33→            BRANCH_TYPE="tag"
    34→          elif [[ "$BRANCH" == "main" ]]; then
    35→            BRANCH_TYPE="main"
    36→          elif [[ "$BRANCH" == "develop" ]]; then
    37→            BRANCH_TYPE="develop"
    38→          else
    39→            echo "::error::Deployments not allowed from branch: $BRANCH"
    40→            echo "::error::Allowed branches: main, develop, v* tags"
    41→            exit 1
    42→          fi
    43→
    44→          case "$BRANCH_TYPE" in
    45→            tag)
    46→              if [[ "$ENV" != "prod" ]]; then
    47→                echo "::error::Version tags (v*) can only deploy to production"
    48→                echo "::error::Current: $BRANCH -> $ENV"
    49→                exit 1
    50→              fi
    51→              ;;
    52→            main)
    53→              if [[ "$ENV" != "dev" && "$ENV" != "qa" ]]; then
    54→                echo "::error::Main branch can deploy to: dev, qa (not $ENV)"
    55→                echo "::error::For production, create a version tag"
    56→                exit 1
    57→              fi
    58→              ;;
    59→            develop)
    60→              if [[ "$ENV" != "dev" ]]; then
    61→                echo "::error::Develop branch can only deploy to dev environment"
    62→                echo "::error::Current: $BRANCH -> $ENV"
    63→                exit 1
    64→              fi
    65→              ;;
    66→          esac
    67→
    68→          echo "Branch-environment check passed: $BRANCH -> $ENV"
    69→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
    70→          echo "## Access Control" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
    71→          echo "- **Branch:** $BRANCH ($BRANCH_TYPE)" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
    72→          echo "- **Environment:** $ENV" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
    73→          echo "- **Status:** Authorized" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
    74→
    75→  deploy:
    76→    name: Deploy to ${{ inputs.environment }}
    77→    needs: check-branch
    78→    runs-on: static-ip-runner
    79→    outputs:
    80→      app_name: ${{ steps.deploy.outputs.app_name }}
    81→      sync_status: ${{ steps.deploy.outputs.sync_status }}
    82→    steps:
    83→      - name: Checkout code
    84→        uses: actions/checkout@v4
    85→
    86→      - name: Install ArgoCD CLI
    87→        run: |
    88→          curl -sSL -o argocd https://github.com/argoproj/argo-cd/releases/latest/download/argocd-linux-amd64
    89→          chmod +x argocd
    90→          sudo mv argocd /usr/local/bin/
    91→
    92→      - name: Deploy ArgoCD Application
    93→        id: deploy
    94→        env:
    95→          ARGOCD_AUTH_TOKEN: ${{ secrets.ARGOCD_AUTH_TOKEN }}
    96→        run: |
    97→          ARGOCD_SERVER="${{ secrets.ARGOCD_SERVER }}"
    98→          ARGOCD_OPTS="--server ${ARGOCD_SERVER} --auth-token ${ARGOCD_AUTH_TOKEN} --grpc-web"
    99→
   100→          APP_FILE="environments/${{ inputs.environment }}/argocd/root-application.yaml"
   101→
   102→          if [ ! -f "$APP_FILE" ]; then
   103→            echo "::error::Application file not found: $APP_FILE"
   104→            exit 1
   105→          fi
   106→
   107→          # Extract app name from the YAML
   108→          APP_NAME=$(grep -E '^\s+name:' "$APP_FILE" | head -1 | awk '{print $2}')
   109→          echo "app_name=$APP_NAME" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   110→
   111→          echo "Deploying application: $APP_NAME"
   112→          echo "Application file: $APP_FILE"
   113→          echo "ArgoCD Server: $ARGOCD_SERVER"
   114→
   115→          # Apply the application manifest
   116→          argocd app create -f "$APP_FILE" --upsert $ARGOCD_OPTS
   117→
   118→          # Sync the application
   119→          argocd app sync "$APP_NAME" $ARGOCD_OPTS --prune --force
   120→
   121→          # Wait for sync to complete
   122→          argocd app wait "$APP_NAME" $ARGOCD_OPTS --timeout 300
   123→
   124→          # Get sync status
   125→          SYNC_STATUS=$(argocd app get "$APP_NAME" $ARGOCD_OPTS -o json | jq -r '.status.sync.status')
   126→          echo "sync_status=$SYNC_STATUS" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
   127→
   128→          echo "Deployment complete. Sync status: $SYNC_STATUS"
   129→
   130→  summary:
   131→    name: Deployment Summary
   132→    needs: [check-branch, deploy]
   133→    if: always()
   134→    runs-on: static-ip-runner
   135→    steps:
   136→      - name: Generate Summary
   137→        run: |
   138→          echo "## ArgoCD Deployment Pipeline" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   139→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   140→
   141→          OVERALL_STATUS="Deployed"
   142→          if [ "${{ needs.deploy.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
   143→            OVERALL_STATUS="Failed"
   144→          elif [ "${{ needs.check-branch.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
   145→            OVERALL_STATUS="Unauthorized"
   146→          elif [ "${{ needs.deploy.result }}" == "skipped" ]; then
   147→            OVERALL_STATUS="Skipped"
   148→          fi
   149→
   150→          echo "### Overall Status: $OVERALL_STATUS" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   151→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   152→          echo "**Environment:** ${{ inputs.environment }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   153→          echo "**Application:** \`${{ needs.deploy.outputs.app_name || 'N/A' }}\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   154→          echo "**Sync Status:** ${{ needs.deploy.outputs.sync_status || 'N/A' }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   155→          echo "**Branch:** ${{ github.ref_name }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   156→          echo "**Triggered by:** @${{ github.actor }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   157→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   158→
   159→          echo "**Job Results:**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   160→          echo "- Branch Check: ${{ needs.check-branch.result }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   161→          echo "- Deploy: ${{ needs.deploy.result }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   162→
   163→      - name: Check Overall Status
   164→        if: needs.deploy.result == 'failure'
   165→        run: |
   166→          echo "::error::ArgoCD deployment failed. Check deploy job logs."
   167→          exit 1
   168→

```json
{
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta/konecta-tic/kix-services-crewai/docs/PATTERN_COMPARISON_AND_IMPLEMENTATION.md",
  "content": "# Pattern Comparison: Kong, Backstage, and CrewAI\n\n**Date:** February 10, 2026\n**Purpose:** Compare deployment patterns across KIX services to identify best practices for CrewAI simplification\n\n---\n\n## Executive Summary\n\n| Aspect | Kong | Backstage | CrewAI (Current) |\n|--------|------|-----------|------------------|\n| **ArgoCD Sources** | Single (local chart) | Single (local chart) | Multi (OCI + local + values) |\n| **ESO Pattern** | None (DBless) | Terraform-managed ExternalSecrets | Helm-templated (dev only) |\n| **SecretStore** | N/A | ClusterSecretStore | Namespace SecretStore |\n| **K8s Secret Creation** | N/A | ESO only | Terraform + ESO (dual path) |\n| **Deploy Workflow** | Reusable | Reusable | Inline |\n| **Environment Parity** | High | High | Low (dev differs from QA/Prod) |\n\n**Recommendation:** Adopt the Backstage pattern - Terraform creates ExternalSecrets, use ClusterSecretStore, migrate to reusable workflow.\n\n---\n\n## Pattern Breakdown\n\n### 1. Kong Gateway Pattern\n\n**Repository:** `konecta-ix-services/kix-services-kong`\n\n**Characteristics:**\n- **No secrets required** - DBless mode (config in YAML, no database)\n- **Single-source ArgoCD** - Local helm chart only\n- **Consistent across environments** - Same chart structure in dev/qa/prod\n- **Minimal Terraform** - Only namespace and IAM\n\n**ArgoCD Application:**\n```yaml\nsource:\n  repoURL: https://github.com/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-kong\n  targetRevision: main\n  path: environments/dev/helm/kong\n  helm:\n    valueFiles:\n      - values.yaml\n      - values-dev.yaml\n```\n\n**Key Insight:** Simplest pattern when secrets aren't needed. Good model for ArgoCD structure and workflow.\n\n---\n\n### 2. Backstage Pattern (RECOMMENDED)\n\n**Repository:** `konecta-ix-services/kix-services-backstage`\n\n**Characteristics:**\n- **Terraform creates ExternalSecrets** via `kubernetes_manifest`\n- **Uses ClusterSecretStore** - Centralized, platform-managed\n- **Single-source ArgoCD** - Local helm chart only\n- **Clear separation** - Terraform = infra/secrets, Helm = workload\n- **Uses reusable workflow** from `.github-private`\n\n**ESO Implementation (Terraform):**\n```hcl\nresource \"kubernetes_manifest\" \"external_secret_iap_oauth\" {\n  manifest = {\n    apiVersion = \"external-secrets.io/v1beta1\"\n    kind       = \"ExternalSecret\"\n    metadata = {\n      name      = \"backstage-iap-oauth\"\n      namespace = var.backstage_namespace\n    }\n    spec = {\n      refreshInterval = \"1h\"\n      secretStoreRef = {\n        kind = \"ClusterSecretStore\"\n        name = \"gcp-secret-manager\"    # Platform-managed, already exists\n      }\n      target = {\n        name           = \"backstage-iap-oauth\"\n        creationPolicy = \"Owner\"\n      }\n      data = [\n        {\n          secretKey = \"client_secret\"\n          remoteRef = {\n            key = var.iap_oauth_secret_name\n          }\n        }\n      ]\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\n**ArgoCD Application:**\n```yaml\nsource:\n  repoURL: https://github.com/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-backstage\n  targetRevision: main\n  path: environments/dev/helm/backstage\n  helm:\n    valueFiles:\n      - values.yaml\n      - values-dev.yaml\n```\n\n**Deploy Workflow (Reusable):**\n```yaml\ndeploy:\n  name: Deploy to ${{ inputs.environment }}\n  needs: check-branch\n  uses: konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/argocd-deploy.yml@main\n  with:\n    application_file: environments/${{ inputs.environment }}/argocd/application.yaml\n  secrets:\n    argocd_auth_token: ${{ secrets.ARGOCD_AUTH_TOKEN }}\n```\n\n**Key Insights:**\n1. ExternalSecrets in Terraform = applied before ArgoCD syncs\n2. ClusterSecretStore = no need for per-namespace SecretStore setup\n3. Single source = simpler sync, fewer moving parts\n4. Reusable workflow = consistent deployment across all services\n\n---\n\n### 3. CrewAI Current Pattern (PROBLEMATIC)\n\n**Repository:** `konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai`\n\n**Issues Identified:**\n\n#### A. Dual Secret Creation Path\n```\nTerraform creates: kubernetes_secret.crewai_secrets (directly)\n    AND\nHelm templates create: ExternalSecret → K8s Secret (same data)\n```\n\nThis creates race conditions and confusion about source of truth.\n\n#### B. Multi-Source ArgoCD Application\n```yaml\nsources:\n  - repoURL: registry.crewai.com/crewai/stable  # OCI chart\n    chart: crewai-platform\n  - repoURL: https://github.com/...              # Local chart\n    path: environments/dev/helm/crewai\n  - repoURL: https://github.com/...              # Values reference\n    ref: values\n```\n\nThree sources = complex sync, difficult debugging, ordering issues.\n\n#### C. Namespace-Scoped SecretStore (Helm)\n```yaml\n# secretstore.yaml (created by Helm)\nkind: SecretStore\nmetadata:\n  name: crewai-gateway-gcp-sm\n  annotations:\n    argocd.argoproj.io/hook: PreSync\n    argocd.argoproj.io/sync-wave: \"-30\"\n```\n\nSecretStore as PreSync hook is fragile - if it fails, ExternalSecrets can't sync.\n\n#### D. Environment Divergence\n| Resource | Dev | QA | Prod |\n|----------|-----|----|----|\n| Local chart | Yes (18 templates) | No | No |\n| ESO | Yes | No | No |\n| Network policies | Yes (Helm) | No | No |\n| Gateway | Yes (Helm) | No | No |\n\nDev cannot validate what runs in production.\n\n#### E. Inline Deploy Workflow\nThe workflow duplicates logic that exists in `.github-private` reusable workflow.\n\n---\n\n## Implementation Plan for CrewAI\n\n### Phase 1: Adopt Backstage ESO Pattern (Week 1)\n\n**Goal:** Move ExternalSecrets from Helm to Terraform, use ClusterSecretStore.\n\n**Step 1.1: Update Terraform to create ExternalSecrets**\n\nAdd to `environments/dev/terraform/secrets.tf`:\n```hcl\n# Use ClusterSecretStore instead of namespace-scoped SecretStore\n# ClusterSecretStore \"gcp-secret-manager\" already exists in cluster\n\nresource \"kubernetes_manifest\" \"external_secret_crewai_credentials\" {\n  manifest = {\n    apiVersion = \"external-secrets.io/v1beta1\"\n    kind       = \"ExternalSecret\"\n    metadata = {\n      name      = \"crewai-credentials\"\n      namespace = local.platform_namespace\n      labels = {\n        app         = \"crewai\"\n        environment = var.environment\n        managed-by  = \"terraform\"\n      }\n    }\n    spec = {\n      refreshInterval = \"1h\"\n      secretStoreRef = {\n        kind = \"ClusterSecretStore\"\n        name = \"gcp-secret-manager\"\n      }\n      target = {\n        name           = \"crewai-credentials\"\n        creationPolicy = \"Owner\"\n      }\n      data = [\n        {\n          secretKey = \"SECRET_KEY_BASE\"\n          remoteRef = {\n            key      = \"crewai-rails-secrets-${var.environment}\"\n            property = \"secret_key_base\"\n          }\n        },\n        {\n          secretKey = \"AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID\"\n          remoteRef = {\n            key      = \"crewai-gcs-credentials-${var.environment}\"\n            property = \"access_key_id\"\n          }\n        },\n        {\n          secretKey = \"AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY\"\n          remoteRef = {\n            key      = \"crewai-gcs-credentials-${var.environment}\"\n            property = \"secret_access_key\"\n          }\n        }\n      ]\n    }\n  }\n  depends_on = [kubernetes_namespace.crewai]\n}\n```\n\n**Step 1.2: Remove direct K8s secret creation from Terraform**\n\nDelete from `secrets.tf`:\n```hcl\n# REMOVE THIS - ESO will manage the secret now\nresource \"kubernetes_secret\" \"crewai_secrets\" {\n  ...\n}\n```\n\n**Step 1.3: Remove ESO templates from Helm chart**\n\nDelete these files from `environments/dev/helm/crewai/templates/`:\n- `secretstore.yaml`\n- `externalsecret.yaml`\n- `externalsecret-postgres.yaml`\n\n**Step 1.4: Update Helm values**\n\nRemove ESO configuration from values.yaml:\n```yaml\n# REMOVE this entire section\nexternalSecrets:\n  enabled: true\n  ...\n```\n\n**Step 1.5: Apply to QA and Prod**\n\nCopy the same Terraform pattern to QA and Prod environments.\n\n---\n\n### Phase 2: Simplify ArgoCD Application (Week 2)\n\n**Goal:** Migrate from multi-source to single-source pattern.\n\n**Option A: Merge local chart into OCI chart values**\n- Gateway, NetworkPolicy → Move to Terraform (like Backstage)\n- HTTPRoute, HealthCheckPolicy → Move to Terraform\n\n**Option B: Keep local chart, remove OCI multi-source**\n- Create comprehensive local chart that wraps OCI chart as subchart\n- Single source to git repo\n\n**Recommended: Option A** (matches Backstage pattern)\n\n**Step 2.1: Move Gateway resources to Terraform**\n\nAdd to `environments/dev/terraform/gateway.tf`:\n```hcl\nresource \"kubernetes_manifest\" \"gateway\" {\n  manifest = {\n    apiVersion = \"gateway.networking.k8s.io/v1\"\n    kind       = \"Gateway\"\n    metadata = {\n      name      = \"crewai-gateway\"\n      namespace = local.platform_namespace\n      annotations = {\n        \"networking.gke.io/certmap\" = google_certificate_manager_certificate_map.crewai.name\n      }\n    }\n    spec = {\n      gatewayClassName = \"gke-l7-global-external-managed\"\n      listeners = [\n        {\n          name     = \"https\"\n          port     = 443\n          protocol = \"HTTPS\"\n        }\n      ]\n    }\n  }\n}\n\nresource \"kubernetes_manifest\" \"httproute\" {\n  manifest = {\n    apiVersion = \"gateway.networking.k8s.io/v1\"\n    kind       = \"HTTPRoute\"\n    metadata = {\n      name      = \"crewai-route\"\n      namespace = local.platform_namespace\n    }\n    spec = {\n      parentRefs = [{\n        name = \"crewai-gateway\"\n      }]\n      hostnames = [var.gateway_hostname]\n      rules = [{\n        backendRefs = [{\n          name = \"crewai-web\"\n          port = 80\n        }]\n      }]\n    }\n  }\n  depends_on = [kubernetes_manifest.gateway]\n}\n```\n\n**Step 2.2: Simplify ArgoCD Application**\n\nUpdate `environments/dev/argocd/apps/application.yaml`:\n```yaml\napiVersion: argoproj.io/v1alpha1\nkind: Application\nmetadata:\n  name: crewai-dev\n  namespace: argocd\nspec:\n  project: platform\n\n  # SINGLE SOURCE - OCI chart only\n  source:\n    repoURL: oci://registry.crewai.com/crewai/stable\n    chart: crewai-platform\n    targetRevision: 0.3.13\n    helm:\n      releaseName: crewai-platform\n      valueFiles:\n        - $values/environments/dev/helm/values.yaml\n\n  # Use git ref for values\n  sourceHydrator:\n    hydrators:\n      - name: values\n        ref: https://github.com/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai\n        targetRevision: main\n\n  destination:\n    server: https://kubernetes.default.svc\n    namespace: crewai\n```\n\n---\n\n### Phase 3: Migrate to Reusable Workflow (Week 2)\n\n**Goal:** Use the shared `.github-private` ArgoCD deploy workflow.\n\n**Update `.github/workflows/argocd-deploy.yml`:**\n\n```yaml\nname: Deploy ArgoCD Application\n\non:\n  workflow_dispatch:\n    inputs:\n      environment:\n        description: 'Target environment'\n        required: true\n        type: choice\n        options:\n          - dev\n          - qa\n          - prod\n\nconcurrency:\n  group: argocd-deploy-${{ inputs.environment }}\n  cancel-in-progress: false\n\npermissions:\n  contents: read\n\njobs:\n  check-branch:\n    name: Check Branch and Environment\n    runs-on: static-ip-runner\n    steps:\n      - name: Validate branch-environment combination\n        run: |\n          BRANCH=\"${{ github.ref_name }}\"\n          ENV=\"${{ inputs.environment }}\"\n\n          if [[ \"$BRANCH\" =~ ^v[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+$ ]]; then\n            BRANCH_TYPE=\"tag\"\n          elif [[ \"$BRANCH\" == \"main\" ]]; then\n            BRANCH_TYPE=\"main\"\n          elif [[ \"$BRANCH\" == \"develop\" ]]; then\n            BRANCH_TYPE=\"develop\"\n          else\n            echo \"::error::Deployments not allowed from branch: $BRANCH\"\n            exit 1\n          fi\n\n          case \"$BRANCH_TYPE\" in\n            tag)\n              [[ \"$ENV\" != \"prod\" ]] && exit 1\n              ;;\n            main)\n              [[ \"$ENV\" != \"dev\" && \"$ENV\" != \"qa\" ]] && exit 1\n              ;;\n            develop)\n              [[ \"$ENV\" != \"dev\" ]] && exit 1\n              ;;\n          esac\n\n  deploy:\n    name: Deploy to ${{ inputs.environment }}\n    needs: check-branch\n    uses: konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/argocd-deploy.yml@main\n    with:\n      application_file: environments/${{ inputs.environment }}/argocd/application.yaml\n    secrets:\n      argocd_auth_token: ${{ secrets.ARGOCD_AUTH_TOKEN }}\n\n  summary:\n    name: Deployment Summary\n    needs: [check-branch, deploy]\n    if: always()\n    runs-on: static-ip-runner\n    steps:\n      - name: Generate Summary\n        run: |\n          echo \"## ArgoCD Deployment\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"**Environment:** ${{ inputs.environment }}\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"**App:** ${{ needs.deploy.outputs.app_name }}\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"**Status:** ${{ needs.deploy.outputs.sync_status }}\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n```\n\n**Key Changes:**\n1. Use reusable workflow instead of inline logic\n2. Point to `application.yaml` not `root-application.yaml` (eliminating app-of-apps)\n3. Consistent with Kong and Backstage patterns\n\n---\n\n### Phase 4: Align Environments (Week 3)\n\n**Goal:** Make dev, QA, and Prod structurally identical.\n\n**Step 4.1: Replicate Terraform ESO pattern to all environments**\n\nEach environment gets:\n- `kubernetes_manifest.external_secret_crewai_credentials`\n- `kubernetes_manifest.gateway`\n- `kubernetes_manifest.httproute`\n- Same module structure\n\n**Step 4.2: Standardize Helm values structure**\n\n```\nenvironments/\n├── dev/\n│   └── helm/\n│       └── values.yaml        # Environment-specific overrides only\n├── qa/\n│   └── helm/\n│       └── values.yaml\n└── prod/\n    └── helm/\n        └── values.yaml\n```\n\n**Step 4.3: Remove local Helm chart from dev**\n\nDelete `environments/dev/helm/crewai/` directory entirely once Gateway/ESO moved to Terraform.\n\n---\n\n## Summary: Target State\n\nAfter implementation, CrewAI will match the Backstage pattern:\n\n| Aspect | Before | After |\n|--------|--------|-------|\n| **ESO Management** | Helm (dev only) | Terraform (all envs) |\n| **SecretStore** | Namespace-scoped | ClusterSecretStore |\n| **K8s Secret** | Terraform direct | ESO-managed |\n| **ArgoCD Sources** | 3 (multi-source) | 1 (single-source) |\n| **Local Chart** | Yes (dev only) | No |\n| **App-of-Apps** | Yes (1:1) | No |\n| **Deploy Workflow** | Inline | Reusable |\n| **Environment Parity** | Low | High |\n\n---\n\n## Benefits\n\n1. **Simpler debugging** - Single source, clear ownership\n2. **Consistent environments** - Same pattern dev→QA→prod\n3. **Reusable patterns** - Shared workflows, less maintenance\n4. **Clear separation** - Terraform = infra, Helm = workload\n5. **Platform alignment** - Matches Kong and Backstage standards\n\n---\n\n## Risks and Mitigations\n\n| Risk | Mitigation |\n|------|------------|\n| Secret sync timing | Terraform applies before ArgoCD; ESO is async but fast |\n| ClusterSecretStore missing | Verify exists: `kubectl get clustersecretstore gcp-secret-manager` |\n| ArgoCD drift during migration | Use `argocd app sync --prune=false` initially |\n| Workflow secret missing | Verify `ARGOCD_AUTH_TOKEN` exists in repo secrets |\n"
}
```

> AGENT

## Analysis Complete

I've reviewed both repositories and created a comprehensive comparison at [PATTERN_COMPARISON_AND_IMPLEMENTATION.md](docs/PATTERN_COMPARISON_AND_IMPLEMENTATION.md).

### Key Findings

#### Kong Pattern (kix-services-kong)
- **No ESO** - Uses DBless mode (no secrets needed)
- **Single-source ArgoCD** - Local helm chart only
- **Uses reusable workflow** from `.github-private`

#### Backstage Pattern (kix-services-backstage) - RECOMMENDED
- **Terraform creates ExternalSecrets** via `kubernetes_manifest`
- **Uses ClusterSecretStore** - Centralized, already exists in cluster
- **Single-source ArgoCD** - Local helm chart only
- **Clear separation**: Terraform = infra/secrets, Helm = workload only

```hcl
# Backstage approach - ExternalSecret in Terraform
resource "kubernetes_manifest" "external_secret_iap_oauth" {
  manifest = {
    apiVersion = "external-secrets.io/v1beta1"
    kind       = "ExternalSecret"
    spec = {
      secretStoreRef = {
        kind = "ClusterSecretStore"
        name = "gcp-secret-manager"  # Platform-managed, no per-namespace setup
      }
      ...
    }
  }
}
```

#### CrewAI Current Issues
1. **Dual secret path** - Terraform creates K8s secret directly AND Helm creates ESO templates
2. **Namespace-scoped SecretStore** - Created by Helm as PreSync hook (fragile)
3. **Multi-source ArgoCD** - 3 sources (OCI + local + values ref)
4. **Dev-only ESO** - QA/Prod don't have it

### Recommended Migration Path

| Phase | Actions | Timeline |
|-------|---------|----------|
| **1** | Move ExternalSecrets from Helm to Terraform, use ClusterSecretStore | Week 1 |
| **2** | Simplify to single-source ArgoCD, move Gateway to Terraform | Week 2 |
| **3** | Migrate to reusable workflow from `.github-private` | Week 2 |
| **4** | Align all environments (dev/QA/prod same structure) | Week 3 |

### ArgoCD Deploy Workflow

CrewAI already has [argocd-deploy.yml](.github/workflows/argocd-deploy.yml) but it uses inline logic. Backstage and Kong use the **reusable workflow**:

```yaml
# Current (inline)
- run: argocd app create -f "$APP_FILE" --upsert $ARGOCD_OPTS

# Recommended (reusable)
uses: konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/argocd-deploy.yml@main
with:
  application_file: environments/${{ inputs.environment }}/argocd/application.yaml
secrets:
  argocd_auth_token: ${{ secrets.ARGOCD_AUTH_TOKEN }}
```

### Target State

| Aspect | Before | After |
|--------|--------|-------|
| ESO Management | Helm templates (dev only) | Terraform (all envs) |
| SecretStore | Namespace-scoped | ClusterSecretStore |
| ArgoCD Sources | 3 (multi-source) | 1 (single-source) |
| Deploy Workflow | Inline | Reusable |

The full implementation guide with code examples is in the docs directory.

```
     1→name: Deploy ArgoCD Application
     2→
     3→on:
     4→  workflow_dispatch:
     5→    inputs:
     6→      environment:
     7→        description: 'Target environment'
     8→        required: true
     9→        type: choice
    10→        options:
    11→          - dev
    12→          - qa
    13→          - prod
    14→
    15→concurrency:
    16→  group: argocd-deploy-${{ inputs.environment }}
    17→  cancel-in-progress: false
    18→
    19→permissions:
    20→  contents: read
    21→
    22→jobs:
    23→  check-branch:
    24→    name: Check Branch and Environment
    25→    runs-on: static-ip-runner
    26→    steps:
    27→      - name: Validate branch-environment combination
    28→        run: |
    29→          BRANCH="${{ github.ref_name }}"
    30→          ENV="${{ inputs.environment }}"
    31→
    32→          if [[ "$BRANCH" =~ ^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$ ]]; then
    33→            BRANCH_TYPE="tag"
    34→          elif [[ "$BRANCH" == "main" ]]; then
    35→            BRANCH_TYPE="main"
    36→          elif [[ "$BRANCH" == "develop" ]]; then
    37→            BRANCH_TYPE="develop"
    38→          else
    39→            echo "::error::Deployments not allowed from branch: $BRANCH"
    40→            echo "::error::Allowed branches: main, develop, v* tags"
    41→            exit 1
    42→          fi
    43→
    44→          case "$BRANCH_TYPE" in
    45→            tag)
    46→              if [[ "$ENV" != "prod" ]]; then
    47→                echo "::error::Version tags (v*) can only deploy to production"
    48→                echo "::error::Current: $BRANCH -> $ENV"
    49→                exit 1
    50→              fi
    51→              ;;
    52→            main)
    53→              if [[ "$ENV" != "dev" && "$ENV" != "qa" ]]; then
    54→                echo "::error::Main branch can deploy to: dev, qa (not $ENV)"
    55→                echo "::error::For production, create a version tag"
    56→                exit 1
    57→              fi
    58→              ;;
    59→            develop)
    60→              if [[ "$ENV" != "dev" ]]; then
    61→                echo "::error::Develop branch can only deploy to dev environment"
    62→                echo "::error::Current: $BRANCH -> $ENV"
    63→                exit 1
    64→              fi
    65→              ;;
    66→          esac
    67→
    68→          echo "Branch-environment check passed: $BRANCH -> $ENV"
    69→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
    70→          echo "## Access Control" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
    71→          echo "- **Branch:** $BRANCH ($BRANCH_TYPE)" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
    72→          echo "- **Environment:** $ENV" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
    73→          echo "- **Status:** Authorized" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
    74→
    75→  deploy:
    76→    name: Deploy to ${{ inputs.environment }}
    77→    needs: check-branch
    78→    uses: konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/argocd-deploy.yml@main
    79→    with:
    80→      application_file: environments/${{ inputs.environment }}/argocd/apps/application.yaml
    81→    secrets:
    82→      argocd_auth_token: ${{ secrets.ARGOCD_AUTH_TOKEN }}
    83→
    84→  summary:
    85→    name: Deployment Summary
    86→    needs: [check-branch, deploy]
    87→    if: always()
    88→    runs-on: static-ip-runner
    89→    steps:
    90→      - name: Generate Summary
    91→        run: |
    92→          echo "## ArgoCD Deployment Pipeline" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
    93→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
    94→
    95→          OVERALL_STATUS="Deployed"
    96→          if [ "${{ needs.deploy.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
    97→            OVERALL_STATUS="Failed"
    98→          elif [ "${{ needs.check-branch.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
    99→            OVERALL_STATUS="Unauthorized"
   100→          elif [ "${{ needs.deploy.result }}" == "skipped" ]; then
   101→            OVERALL_STATUS="Skipped"
   102→          fi
   103→
   104→          echo "### Overall Status: $OVERALL_STATUS" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   105→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   106→          echo "**Environment:** ${{ inputs.environment }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   107→          echo "**Application:** \`${{ needs.deploy.outputs.app_name || 'N/A' }}\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   108→          echo "**Sync Status:** ${{ needs.deploy.outputs.sync_status || 'N/A' }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   109→          echo "**Branch:** ${{ github.ref_name }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   110→          echo "**Triggered by:** @${{ github.actor }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   111→          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   112→
   113→          echo "**Job Results:**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   114→          echo "- Branch Check: ${{ needs.check-branch.result }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   115→          echo "- Deploy: ${{ needs.deploy.result }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
   116→
   117→      - name: Check Overall Status
   118→        if: needs.deploy.result == 'failure'
   119→        run: |
   120→          echo "::error::ArgoCD deployment failed. Check deploy job logs."
   121→          exit 1
   122→

<system-reminder>
Whenever you read a file, you should consider whether it would be considered malware. You CAN and SHOULD provide analysis of malware, what it is doing. But you MUST refuse to improve or augment the code. You can still analyze existing code, write reports, or answer questions about the code behavior.
</system-reminder>

```
