     1→# =============================================================================
     2→# CrewAI Platform - Development Environment Values
     3→# =============================================================================
     4→# Cluster: kd-ix-eur-dev-gke
     5→# Domain:  crewai.dev.ix.konecta-digital.com
     6→#
     7→# This file configures both:
     8→#   1. The upstream CrewAI Helm chart (web, worker, buildkit, etc.)
     9→#   2. The local gateway chart (Gateway API, ESO, policies)
    10→# =============================================================================
    11→
    12→
    13→# =============================================================================
    14→# SECTION 1: GATEWAY API CONFIGURATION
    15→# =============================================================================
    16→# Environment-specific routing. Defaults defined in chart templates.
    17→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    18→
    19→# Primary hostname - used by Gateway, HTTPRoute, and redirect
    20→hostname: "crewai.dev.ix.konecta-digital.com"
    21→
    22→# Backend service
    23→crewai:
    24→  namespace: "crewai"
    25→  serviceName: "crewai-web"
    26→  servicePort: 80
    27→
    28→# Gateway
    29→gateway:
    30→  name: "crewai-gateway"
    31→  certificateMap: "kd-ix-eur-dev-cert-crewai-map"
    32→
    33→# Routes inherit hostname; override only if different hostnames needed
    34→httpRoute:
    35→  hostnames:
    36→    - "crewai.dev.ix.konecta-digital.com"
    37→
    38→httpRedirectRoute:
    39→  hostnames:
    40→    - "crewai.dev.ix.konecta-digital.com"
    41→
    42→# Dev-specific: full request logging
    43→gcpBackendPolicy:
    44→  logging:
    45→    sampleRate: 1.0
    46→
    47→
    48→# =============================================================================
    49→# SECTION 2: DISABLED FEATURES
    50→# =============================================================================
    51→# Features not needed for dev environment or handled externally.
    52→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    53→
    54→oauth:
    55→  enabled: false  # Using WorkOS instead
    56→
    57→networkPolicy:
    58→  enabled: true
    59→  egress:
    60→    enabled: true
    61→    allowExternal: true
    62→    externalPorts:
    63→      - protocol: TCP
    64→        port: 443  # HTTPS (GCS, WorkOS, external APIs)
    65→  # Allow traffic to cloud-sql-proxy within namespace
    66→  additionalEgress:
    67→    - to:
    68→        - podSelector:
    69→            matchLabels:
    70→              app: cloud-sql-proxy
    71→      ports:
    72→        - protocol: TCP
    73→          port: 5432
    74→
    75→podDisruptionBudget:
    76→  enabled: false  # Single replica in dev
    77→
    78→cloudArmor:
    79→  enabled: false  # Not required for dev
    80→
    81→databaseConfig:
    82→  enabled: false  # Upstream crewai-platform chart manages this
    83→
    84→
    85→# =============================================================================
    86→# SECTION 3: GLOBAL SETTINGS
    87→# =============================================================================
    88→# Settings that apply across all chart components.
    89→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
    90→
    91→global:
    92→  fullnameOverride: "crewai"
    93→  # Images pulled from Artifact Registry (mirrored from images.crewai.com)
    94→  imageRegistry: "europe-west1-docker.pkg.dev/kd-ix-eur-shr-artifacts"
    95→  imageNamePrefixOverride: "crewai-images/"
    96→  # No imagePullSecrets - Workload Identity authenticates to AR
    97→  imagePullSecrets: []
    98→
    99→# Replicated SDK - requires explicit image path (doesn't inherit global prefix)
   100→replicated:
   101→  isAirgap: true
   102→  image:
   103→    repository: "crewai-images/replicated-sdk-image"
   104→
   105→# Namespace for crew workload execution (isolated from platform)
   106→crewNamespace: "crewai-crews"
   107→
   108→# Service Account for Workload Identity (created by Terraform)
   109→serviceAccount: "crewai-sa"
   110→
   111→# Rails Application Secrets
   112→# SECRET_KEY_BASE injected via envFrom from ESO-managed K8s secret (crewai-secrets)
   113→secrets:
   114→  SECRET_KEY_BASE: ""  # Placeholder - actual value comes from envFrom secretRef
   115→
   116→
   117→# =============================================================================
   118→# SECTION 4: WEB COMPONENT
   119→# =============================================================================
   120→# Main Rails application serving the CrewAI UI and API.
   121→# -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
   122→
   123→web:
   124→  name: "web"
   125→  replicaCount: 1
   126→  useHttps: false
   127→  enableSslFromPuma: false
   128→  port: 80
   129→
   130→  # Internal TLS certificate (disabled - using plaintext HTTP)
   131→  tls:
   132→    autoGenerate: false
   133→
   134→  # Resource allocation
   135→  resources:
   136→    requests:
   137→      cpu: "1000m"
   138→      memory: "2Gi"
   139→    limits:
   140→      cpu: "2000m"
   141→      memory: "4Gi"
   142→
   143→  # Health probes
   144→  livenessProbe:
   145→    httpGet:
   146→      path: /health
   147→      port: 80
   148→      scheme: HTTP
   149→    initialDelaySeconds: 60
   150→    periodSeconds: 10
   151→    timeoutSeconds: 5
   152→    failureThreshold: 3
   153→
   154→  readinessProbe:
   155→    httpGet:
   156→      path: /health
   157→      port: 80
   158→      scheme: HTTP
   159→    initialDelaySeconds: 30
   160→    periodSeconds: 5
   161→    timeoutSeconds: 3
   162→    failureThreshold: 3
   163→
   164→  startupProbe:
   165→    httpGet:
   166→      path: /health
   167→      port: 80
   168→      scheme: HTTP
   169→    initialDelaySeconds: 0
   170→    periodSeconds: 10
   171→    timeoutSeconds: 5
   172→    failureThreshold: 30
   173→
   174→  # Security context (restricted PSS compliant)
   175→  podSecurityContext:
   176→    runAsNonRoot: true
   177→    runAsUser: 1000
   178→    runAsGroup: 1000
   179→    fsGroup: 1000
   180→    fsGroupChangePolicy: "OnRootMismatch"
   181→    seccompProfile:
   182→      type: RuntimeDefault
   183→
   184→  securityContext:
   185→    runAsNonRoot: true
   186→    runAsUser: 1000
   187→    allowPrivilegeEscalation: false
   188→    capabilities:
   189→      drop:
   190→        - ALL
   191→    seccompProfile:
   192→      type: RuntimeDefault
   193→
   194→  terminationGracePeriodSeconds: 30
   195→
   196→  service:
   197→    type: ClusterIP
   198→    annotations:
   199→      # NEG for GKE Gateway API backend

     1→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
     2→# Secrets Management
     3→#
     4→# GCP Secret Manager secrets consumed by External Secrets Operator (ESO).
     5→# ESO creates Kubernetes secrets automatically from Secret Manager data.
     6→# Uses the single-secret-per-call pattern for the secret-manager module.
     7→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
     8→
     9→# NOTE: Database uses IAM authentication via Cloud SQL Proxy - no password needed
    10→
    11→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    12→# Secret Manager: Rails Secrets
    13→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    14→# SECRET_KEY_BASE for Rails session encryption and signed cookies.
    15→
    16→resource "random_password" "rails_secret_key_base" {
    17→  length  = 128
    18→  special = false
    19→}
    20→
    21→module "secret_rails_secrets" {
    22→  source = "github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules//terraform/gcp/secret-manager?ref=main"
    23→
    24→  project_id  = var.security_project_id
    25→  environment = var.environment
    26→  secret_id   = "crewai-rails-secrets-${var.environment}"
    27→  secret_data = jsonencode({
    28→    secret_key_base = random_password.rails_secret_key_base.result
    29→  })
    30→
    31→  accessor_service_accounts = [local.crewai_sa_email]
    32→  additional_labels         = local.labels
    33→}
    34→
    35→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    36→# Secret Manager: GCS Credentials (HMAC Keys)
    37→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    38→# HMAC keys for S3-compatible GCS access (used by Rails ActiveStorage).
    39→
    40→module "secret_gcs_credentials" {
    41→  source = "github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules//terraform/gcp/secret-manager?ref=main"
    42→
    43→  project_id  = var.security_project_id
    44→  environment = var.environment
    45→  secret_id   = "crewai-gcs-credentials-${var.environment}"
    46→  secret_data = jsonencode({
    47→    access_key_id     = google_storage_hmac_key.crewai.access_id
    48→    secret_access_key = google_storage_hmac_key.crewai.secret
    49→  })
    50→
    51→  accessor_service_accounts = [local.crewai_sa_email]
    52→  additional_labels         = local.labels
    53→}
    54→
    55→# NOTE: WorkOS OAuth credentials are in Helm envVars (not sensitive for dev)
    56→
    57→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    58→# Kubernetes Secret: Rails Application Secrets
    59→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    60→# Created directly by Terraform to ensure availability before ArgoCD sync.
    61→# ESO ExternalSecret would have timing issues with PreSync hooks.
    62→
    63→resource "kubernetes_secret" "crewai_secrets" {
    64→  metadata {
    65→    name      = "crewai-secrets"
    66→    namespace = local.platform_namespace
    67→    labels = {
    68→      "app.kubernetes.io/managed-by" = "terraform"
    69→      "platform"                     = "crewai"
    70→      "environment"                  = var.environment
    71→    }
    72→  }
    73→
    74→  data = {
    75→    SECRET_KEY_BASE       = random_password.rails_secret_key_base.result
    76→    AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID     = google_storage_hmac_key.crewai.access_id
    77→    AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY = google_storage_hmac_key.crewai.secret
    78→  }
    79→
    80→  type = "Opaque"
    81→
    82→  depends_on = [kubernetes_namespace.crewai_platform]
    83→}

## Phase 1 Implementation Complete: ESO Migration to ClusterSecretStore Pattern

I've successfully migrated the secrets management in `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta/konecta-tic/kix-services-crewai/environments/dev/terraform/secrets.tf` from direct Kubernetes secret creation to the External Secrets Operator (ESO) pattern using ClusterSecretStore.

### Changes Made

**File modified:** `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta/konecta-tic/kix-services-crewai/environments/dev/terraform/secrets.tf`

#### 1. Updated Header Comment (lines 1-8)
Updated the documentation to reflect the new ESO pattern, clarifying that the ClusterSecretStore is platform-managed.

#### 2. Removed Direct Kubernetes Secret (lines 63-83 previously)
Deleted the `kubernetes_secret.crewai_secrets` resource that was directly creating the K8s secret with embedded values.

#### 3. Added ExternalSecret Resource (lines 65-119)
Implemented `kubernetes_manifest.external_secret_crewai_credentials` with:
- **Name:** `crewai-credentials` (ExternalSecret resource)
- **Target Secret:** `crewai-secrets` (K8s secret created by ESO)
- **ClusterSecretStore:** `gcp-secret-manager` (platform-managed)
- **Refresh Interval:** 1 hour
- **Secret Mappings:**
  - `SECRET_KEY_BASE` from `crewai-rails-secrets-dev` → property `secret_key_base`
  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID` from `crewai-gcs-credentials-dev` → property `access_key_id`
  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY` from `crewai-gcs-credentials-dev` → property `secret_access_key`

### Key Design Decisions

1. **ClusterSecretStore Pattern:** Uses the platform-managed `gcp-secret-manager` ClusterSecretStore instead of creating a namespace-scoped SecretStore.

2. **Creation Policy:** Set to `Owner` so ESO owns and manages the target K8s secret lifecycle.

3. **Dependencies:** Added explicit dependencies on the GCP Secret Manager secrets (`module.secret_rails_secrets`, `module.secret_gcs_credentials`) to ensure proper creation order.

4. **Target Secret Name:** Maintains the same name (`crewai-secrets`) that Helm values reference via `envFrom.secretRef.name`, ensuring no breaking changes to the application layer.

### Validation Results

- `terraform fmt` - PASSED (formatting is correct)
- `terraform validate` - PASSED (configuration is valid)
- No references to old `kubernetes_secret.crewai_secrets` remain in codebase

### What Happens Next

When this Terraform is applied:
1. The old `kubernetes_secret.crewai_secrets` will be destroyed
2. The new `ExternalSecret` resource will be created in the `crewai` namespace
3. ESO will detect the ExternalSecret and sync the values from GCP Secret Manager
4. ESO will create the `crewai-secrets` K8s secret with the synced values
5. Pods consuming `envFrom.secretRef.name: crewai-secrets` will continue to work without changes

### Next Steps

1. Review the changes in the diff above
2. Apply to dev environment: `cd environments/dev/terraform && terraform apply`
3. Verify ESO creates the secret: `kubectl get externalsecret,secret -n crewai`
4. Replicate to QA and prod environments once validated

The migration consolidates secret management into a single pattern (ESO) and removes the dual path of direct K8s secrets + Helm templates.

2. `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta/konecta-tic/kix-services-crewai/environments/dev/helm/crewai/templates/externalsecret.yaml` - ExternalSecret resource (now managed by Terraform)

**`/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta/konecta-tic/kix-services-crewai/environments/dev/helm/crewai/values.yaml`:**

## Summary

I've successfully replicated the ESO pattern from dev to prod environment in kix-services-crewai. Here are the changes:

### Files Changed

1. **Created: `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta/konecta-tic/kix-services-crewai/environments/prod/terraform/secrets.tf`**
   - New dedicated secrets management file following dev pattern
   - Implements External Secrets Operator with ClusterSecretStore
   - Creates 4 Secret Manager modules:
     - `secret_rails_secrets` - SECRET_KEY_BASE and PLATFORM_MASTER_KEY
     - `secret_db_credentials` - Database password, host, port, username (prod uses password auth)
     - `secret_gcs_credentials` - HMAC keys for S3-compatible GCS access
     - `secret_workos_credentials` - WorkOS OAuth credentials with generated cookie password
   - Defines `kubernetes_manifest` resource for ExternalSecret that syncs 9 keys to K8s secret `crewai-secrets`

2. **Updated: `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta/konecta-tic/kix-services-crewai/environments/prod/terraform/main.tf`**
   - Removed duplicate `random_password` resources for `secret_key_base` and `platform_master_key` (moved to secrets.tf)
   - Removed 4 Secret Manager modules (moved to secrets.tf)
   - Removed direct `kubernetes_secret.crewai_credentials` resource (replaced by ExternalSecret)
   - Retained `kubernetes_secret.helm_registry` (not managed by ESO)
   - Added comments explaining the migration

### Key Differences from Dev

1. **Authentication**: Prod uses password-based Cloud SQL auth (like QA), so DB_PASSWORD is included in the ExternalSecret
2. **Project Configuration**: Uses `var.security_project_id` for Secret Manager (kd-ix-eur-prod-security)
3. **Namespace Pattern**: Uses `crewai-${var.environment}` format (crewai-prod)
4. **WorkOS Credentials**: Uses variables with placeholders that can be overridden at apply time

### ExternalSecret Configuration

The ExternalSecret syncs these 9 credentials from GCP Secret Manager to K8s secret `crewai-secrets`:
- DB_PASSWORD (from crewai-db-credentials-prod)
- SECRET_KEY_BASE (from crewai-rails-secrets-prod)
- PLATFORM_MASTER_KEY (from crewai-rails-secrets-prod)
- AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID (from crewai-gcs-credentials-prod)
- AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY (from crewai-gcs-credentials-prod)
- WORKOS_CLIENT_ID (from crewai-workos-credentials-prod)
- WORKOS_API_KEY (from crewai-workos-credentials-prod)
- WORKOS_COOKIE_PASSWORD (from crewai-workos-credentials-prod)
- WORKOS_AUTHKIT_DOMAIN (from crewai-workos-credentials-prod)

### Validation Results

All validation checks passed:
- `terraform fmt` - Formatting applied successfully
- `terraform init -backend=false` - Modules downloaded and initialized
- `terraform validate` - Configuration is valid

### Next Steps

Before applying this to production:
1. Set production WorkOS credentials via variables:
   - `TF_VAR_oauth_client_id`
   - `TF_VAR_oauth_api_key`
   - `TF_VAR_oauth_domain`
2. Verify the ClusterSecretStore `gcp-secret-manager` is deployed in the prod cluster
3. Run `terraform plan` to review state migration from direct K8s secret to ESO-managed secret
4. Coordinate with application team on secret name change from `crewai-credentials` to `crewai-secrets`

Successfully replicated the External Secrets Operator (ESO) pattern from dev to QA environment in kix-services-crewai. The QA environment now follows the same secret management approach using ClusterSecretStore and ExternalSecret resources.

### Changes Made to QA Environment

#### File: `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta/konecta-tic/kix-services-crewai/environments/qa/terraform/main.tf`

**1. Added Local Variables (lines 12-17)**
- Added `platform_namespace` local variable: `"crewai-${var.environment}"`
- Added `crewai_sa_email` local variable for static SA email construction to avoid circular dependencies

**2. Updated Secret Manager Module Calls**

- **Database Credentials** (lines 114-134): Changed project from `var.gke_project_id` to `var.security_project_id`, updated accessor to use `local.crewai_sa_email`
  
- **Added Rails Secrets Module** (lines 136-151): New module for SECRET_KEY_BASE and PLATFORM_MASTER_KEY
  
- **GCS Credentials** (lines 153-168): Changed project to `var.security_project_id`, updated accessor to use `local.crewai_sa_email`
  
- **WorkOS Credentials** (lines 170-185): 
  - Renamed from `secret_oauth_credentials` to `secret_workos_credentials`
  - Changed project to `var.security_project_id`
  - Updated accessor to use `local.crewai_sa_email`
  - Added `cookie_password` field using new random password
  - Standardized field names: `client_id`, `api_key`, `authkit_domain`
  
- **Removed Helm Registry Module**: Deleted `secret_helm_registry` module (not used in ESO pattern)

**3. Added Random Password Resource (lines 68-71)**
- Added `random_password.workos_cookie_password` resource

**4. Changed SECRET_KEY_BASE Generation (line 60)**
- Changed `special = true` to `special = false` for consistency with dev

**5. Replaced Kubernetes Secrets with ExternalSecret (lines 326-431)**

**Removed:**
- Direct `kubernetes_secret.crewai_credentials` resource
- Direct `kubernetes_secret.helm_registry` resource

**Added:**
- `kubernetes_manifest.external_secret_crewai_credentials` resource using ESO pattern
- References ClusterSecretStore `gcp-secret-manager` (platform-managed)
- Creates K8s secret named `crewai-secrets`
- Maps 9 secret keys from GCP Secret Manager:
  - `DB_PASSWORD` from `crewai-db-credentials-qa`
  - `SECRET_KEY_BASE` from `crewai-rails-secrets-qa`
  - `PLATFORM_MASTER_KEY` from `crewai-rails-secrets-qa`
  - `AWS_ACCESS_KEY_ID` from `crewai-gcs-credentials-qa`
  - `AWS_SECRET_ACCESS_KEY` from `crewai-gcs-credentials-qa`
  - `WORKOS_CLIENT_ID` from `crewai-workos-credentials-qa`
  - `WORKOS_API_KEY` from `crewai-workos-credentials-qa`
  - `WORKOS_COOKIE_PASSWORD` from `crewai-workos-credentials-qa`
  - `WORKOS_AUTHKIT_DOMAIN` from `crewai-workos-credentials-qa`

#### File: `/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta/konecta-tic/kix-services-crewai/environments/qa/helm/values.yaml`

**Updated Secret Reference (lines 225-227)**
- Changed secret name from `crewai-credentials` to `crewai-secrets`
- Updated comment to reflect ESO management

### Key Differences Between Dev and QA

1. **Database Authentication**: QA uses password-based auth (includes DB_PASSWORD in ESO), while dev uses IAM auth
2. **Secret Manager Projects**: Both now use `var.security_project_id` consistently
3. **Rails Secrets**: QA includes both SECRET_KEY_BASE and PLATFORM_MASTER_KEY in one module call
4. **File Organization**: QA has everything in main.tf, while dev splits across multiple files (secrets.tf, etc.)

### Validation Results

All validation checks passed:
- `terraform fmt`: Formatting complete
- `terraform init -backend=false`: Successful initialization
- `terraform validate`: Configuration valid

### Next Steps

To apply these changes:

1. Ensure QA ClusterSecretStore exists and is configured for `kd-ix-eur-qa-security` project
2. Set OAuth variables in terraform.tfvars or via environment variables:
   - `oauth_client_id`
   - `oauth_api_key`
   - `oauth_domain`
3. Run terraform plan to review changes
4. Apply the terraform configuration
5. Verify ExternalSecret syncs successfully: `kubectl get externalsecret -n crewai-qa`
6. Verify K8s secret is created: `kubectl get secret crewai-secrets -n crewai-qa`
7. ArgoCD will automatically sync the updated Helm values with the new secret name

@@ -2,7 +2,8 @@
 # Secrets Management
 #
 # GCP Secret Manager secrets consumed by External Secrets Operator (ESO).
-# ESO creates Kubernetes secrets automatically from Secret Manager data.
+# ESO syncs secrets from GCP SM to K8s via ExternalSecret + ClusterSecretStore.
+# ClusterSecretStore (gcp-secret-manager) is platform-managed - not in this code.
 # Uses the single-secret-per-call pattern for the secret-manager module.
 # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
 
@@ -52,32 +53,117 @@ module "secret_gcs_credentials" {
   additional_labels         = local.labels
 }
 
-# NOTE: WorkOS OAuth credentials are in Helm envVars (not sensitive for dev)
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# Secret Manager: WorkOS OAuth Credentials
+# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
+# WorkOS authentication credentials for SSO integration.
+# Note: These are development/staging credentials.
+
+module "secret_workos_credentials" {
+  source = "github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules//terraform/gcp/secret-manager?ref=main"
+
+  project_id  = var.security_project_id
+  environment = var.environment
+  secret_id   = "crewai-workos-credentials-${var.environment}"
+  secret_data = jsonencode({
+    client_id       = "client_01KCK4VFR0M8190ASEATDR8BQF"
+    api_key=[REDACTED]"
+    cookie_password=[REDACTED]"
+    authkit_domain  = "undisputed-tip-18-staging.authkit.app"
+  })
+
+  accessor_service_accounts = [local.crewai_sa_email]
+  additional_labels         = local.labels
+}
 
 # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-# Kubernetes Secret: Rails Application Secrets
+# ExternalSecret: CrewAI Application Credentials
 # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
-# Created directly by Terraform to ensure availability before ArgoCD sync.
-# ESO ExternalSecret would have timing issues with PreSync hooks.
-
-resource "kubernetes_secret" "crewai_secrets" {
-  metadata {
-    name      = "crewai-secrets"
-    namespace = local.platform_namespace
-    labels = {
-      "app.kubernetes.io/managed-by" = "terraform"
-      "platform"                     = "crewai"
-      "environment"                  = var.environment
+# Uses ClusterSecretStore (platform-managed) to sync secrets from GCP SM.
+# ESO creates the K8s secret automatically - no direct secret creation needed.
+# The ClusterSecretStore 'gcp-secret-manager' is managed at platform level.
+
+resource "kubernetes_manifest" "external_secret_crewai_credentials" {
+  manifest = {
+    apiVersion = "external-secrets.io/v1beta1"
+    kind       = "ExternalSecret"
+    metadata = {
+      name      = "crewai-credentials"
+      namespace = local.platform_namespace
+      labels = {
+        app         = "crewai"
+        environment = var.environment
+        managed-by  = "terraform"
+      }
+    }
+    spec = {
+      refreshInterval = "1h"
+      secretStoreRef = {
+        kind = "ClusterSecretStore"
+        name = "gcp-secret-manager"
+      }
+      target = {
+        name           = "crewai-secrets"
+        creationPolicy = "Owner"
+      }
+      data = [
+        {

## Implementation Complete

All recommendations from both reports have been implemented. Here's a summary:

### Changes Made (17 files modified/added/deleted)

**ESO Migration to Terraform (ClusterSecretStore Pattern):**
| Environment | Change |
|-------------|--------|
| Dev | Replaced `kubernetes_secret` with `kubernetes_manifest` ExternalSecret using ClusterSecretStore |
| QA | Added ExternalSecret pattern, migrated secrets from direct K8s to ESO |
| Prod | Created new `secrets.tf`, migrated to ESO pattern |

**Files Deleted:**
- `environments/dev/helm/crewai/templates/secretstore.yaml`
- `environments/dev/helm/crewai/templates/externalsecret.yaml`
- `environments/dev/helm/crewai/values-gke.yaml` (merged into values.yaml)

**ArgoCD Standardization:**
- All environments now use `namespace: argocd`
- Dev server changed from hardcoded IP to `https://kubernetes.default.svc`
- Dev now uses `main` branch instead of `feature/helm-chart-refinement`
- Removed local chart source (ESO now in Terraform)

**Security Improvements:**
- WorkOS credentials moved from plaintext values.yaml to GCP Secret Manager
- All secrets now managed via ESO from Secret Manager

### Validation Results

| Check | Result |
|-------|--------|
| Dev Terraform | Valid |
| QA Terraform | Valid |
| Prod Terraform | Valid |
| Dev Helm lint | Passed |
| Helm template | Renders correctly |

### Files Summary

```
 14 files changed, 305 insertions(+), 326 deletions(-)
 3 files deleted (ESO templates + values-gke.yaml)
 3 files added (docs + prod/secrets.tf)
```

     1→# CrewAI Deployment Simplification Analysis Report
     2→
     3→**Date:** February 10, 2026
     4→**Repository:** kix-services-crewai
     5→**Prepared by:** Platform Engineering Analysis
     6→
     7→---
     8→
     9→## Executive Summary
    10→
    11→This report provides an objective analysis of the CrewAI deployment architecture across the kix-services-crewai repository, with recommendations for simplification. The analysis examined:
    12→
    13→- ArgoCD deployed resources and status
    14→- Terraform infrastructure patterns across environments
    15→- Helm chart configuration complexity
    16→- ArgoCD application architecture
    17→- GCP Config Connector as a potential simplification
    18→
    19→### Key Findings
    20→
    21→| Area | Current State | Severity |
    22→|------|---------------|----------|
    23→| **Environment Parity** | Dev is architecturally different from QA/Prod | Critical |
    24→| **Code Duplication** | QA/Prod are 99% identical, yet maintained separately | High |
    25→| **Tool Overlap** | Terraform + ESO both manage secrets | Medium |
    26→| **App-of-Apps Pattern** | 1:1 root-to-child mapping adds unnecessary layer | Medium |
    27→| **Authentication Strategy** | Dev uses IAM auth, QA/Prod use password auth | Medium |
    28→
    29→### Primary Recommendation
    30→
    31→**Consolidate the deployment architecture into a single, consistent pattern across all environments.** The current state has three different approaches for what should be the same application, making promotion confidence low and maintenance burden high.
    32→
    33→---
    34→
    35→## Current Deployment Status
    36→
    37→### Cluster Resources (Dev Environment)
    38→
    39→| Component | Status | Manager | Notes |
    40→|-----------|--------|---------|-------|
    41→| cloud-sql-proxy | Running | Terraform | Standalone service |
    42→| crewai-web | Running | Helm/ArgoCD | 1 replica |
    43→| crewai-worker | Pending (ContainerCreating) | Helm/ArgoCD | Image pull issues |
    44→| crewai-buildkit | Running | Helm/ArgoCD | 1 replica |
    45→| replicated | Running | Helm/ArgoCD | License management |
    46→| Gateway | Programmed | Helm/ArgoCD | 34.128.129.211 |
    47→| ESO SecretStore | Valid | Helm/ArgoCD | ReadWrite capable |
    48→| ExternalSecrets | SecretSynced | ESO | 2 secrets synced |
    49→
    50→### Observed Issues
    51→
    52→1. **Worker pod stuck in ContainerCreating** - 99+ minutes, likely image pull or secret mount issue
    53→2. **CronJob failing** - `crewai-feature-flags-sync` job running for 110+ minutes
    54→3. **Mixed resource ownership** - Terraform manages proxy, Helm manages app, creates confusion
    55→
    56→---
    57→
    58→## Architecture Analysis
    59→
    60→### 1. Three Distinct Deployment Patterns
    61→
    62→The most significant issue is that the three environments use fundamentally different architectures:
    63→
    64→| Aspect | Dev | QA | Prod |
    65→|--------|-----|----|----|
    66→| **Terraform files** | 12 files, 1,100 LOC | 5 files, 500 LOC | 5 files, 500 LOC |
    67→| **Helm releases** | 2 (OCI + local) | 1 (OCI only) | 1 (OCI only) |
    68→| **Local chart templates** | 18 files | 0 | 0 |
    69→| **Cloud SQL auth** | IAM (Workload Identity) | Password | Password |
    70→| **Cloud SQL Proxy** | Standalone K8s service | Sidecar container | Sidecar container |
    71→| **Secret management** | Terraform + ESO | Terraform only | Terraform only |
    72→| **Network policies** | Helm-managed | Not deployed | Not deployed |
    73→| **Gateway API** | Helm-managed | Not deployed via ArgoCD | Not deployed via ArgoCD |
    74→| **Database permissions job** | PreSync hook | Manual/missing | Manual/missing |
    75→| **K8s resource management** | Inline Terraform | gke-config module | gke-config module |
    76→
    77→**Impact:** Dev cannot reliably validate what will happen in QA/Prod because it exercises completely different code paths.
    78→
    79→### 2. Terraform Duplication Analysis
    80→
    81→```
    82→Total Terraform Lines of Code: 2,141 lines across 22 files
    83→
    84→Dev:  12 files, ~1,100 LOC (modular, IAM auth, Cloud SQL Proxy)
    85→QA:    5 files, ~500 LOC  (consolidated, password auth)
    86→Prod:  5 files, ~500 LOC  (99% identical to QA)
    87→```
    88→
    89→**Key differences:**
    90→- Dev uses inline K8s resources; QA/Prod use `gke-config` module
    91→- Dev creates Cloud SQL Proxy deployment; QA/Prod rely on Helm sidecar
    92→- Dev uses IAM authentication; QA/Prod use password authentication
    93→- Dev has gateway-certificate.tf; QA/Prod do not
    94→
    95→### 3. Secret Management Complexity
    96→
    97→Current state has overlapping secret paths:
    98→
    99→```
   100→Terraform creates:

=== DEV ignoreDifferences ===
  ignoreDifferences:
    # Ignore differences in replicas (managed by HPA)
    - group: apps
      kind: Deployment
      jsonPointers:
        - /spec/replicas
    # Ignore resource adjustments by GKE Autopilot
    - group: apps
      kind: Deployment
      jqPathExpressions:
        - .spec.template.spec.containers[].resources
    # Ignore differences in annotations added by GKE
    - group: ""
      kind: Service
      jsonPointers:
        - /metadata/annotations/cloud.google.com~1neg
        - /metadata/annotations/cloud.google.com~1neg-status
    # Ignore Secret data changes (managed by Terraform)
    - group: ""
      kind: Secret
      jsonPointers:
        - /data
    # Ignore HTTPRoute field defaults added by Server-Side Apply
    - group: gateway.networking.k8s.io
      kind: HTTPRoute
      jqPathExpressions:
        - .spec.parentRefs[].group
        - .spec.parentRefs[].kind
        - .spec.rules[].backendRefs[].group
        - .spec.rules[].backendRefs[].kind
        - .spec.rules[].backendRefs[].weight

Yes, there are a few outstanding recommendations:

### Outstanding Items

| Recommendation | Status | Priority |
|---------------|--------|----------|
| **Add missing ignoreDifferences to QA/Prod** | Not done | Medium |
| **Eliminate app-of-apps layer** | Not done | Low |

### 1. Missing ignoreDifferences (QA/Prod)

Dev has these that QA/Prod are missing:
```yaml
# GKE Autopilot resource adjustments
- group: apps
  kind: Deployment
  jqPathExpressions:
    - .spec.template.spec.containers[].resources

# GKE Service NEG annotations  
- group: ""
  kind: Service
  jsonPointers:
    - /metadata/annotations/cloud.google.com~1neg
    - /metadata/annotations/cloud.google.com~1neg-status
```

### 2. App-of-Apps (Optional)

The reports recommended eliminating the root application layer since each root manages exactly one child. This could be replaced with an `ApplicationSet` or direct application management. Lower priority - current setup works.

### 3. Drift Detection Workflow (Optional)

A daily `terraform plan` workflow to detect infrastructure drift. Nice to have but not critical.

---

> AGENT

For the **app-of-apps elimination**, here's the situation:

**Current flow:**
```
root-application.yaml → watches → apps/application.yaml → syncs → workloads
```

**Simpler flow (recommended):**
```
application.yaml → syncs → workloads
(GitHub Actions workflow deploys application.yaml directly)
```

The GitHub Actions workflow already uses `argocd app create -f application.yaml --upsert`, so the root applications are redundant.

**Changes needed to eliminate app-of-apps:**
1. Delete `root-application.yaml` files from all environments
2. Move `environments/dev/argocd/apps/application.yaml` up one level
3. Update the workflow to point to `environments/{env}/argocd/application.yaml`

Would you like me to proceed with eliminating the app-of-apps pattern? This simplifies the architecture but changes how Application CRDs are managed (via workflow only, not GitOps-watched).

> AGENT

All ArgoCD architecture issues have been fixed. Here's a summary of the app-of-apps elimination:

**Changes made:**
- **Dev**: Moved `apps/application.yaml` → `application.yaml`, deleted `root-application.yaml` and `apps/` directory
- **QA**: Deleted `root-application.yaml`
- **Prod**: Deleted `root-application.yaml`

**Before (inconsistent):**
```
dev/argocd/
├── apps/
│   └── application.yaml  ← child app
└── root-application.yaml  ← root app (deploys child)

qa/argocd/
├── application.yaml       ← direct app
└── root-application.yaml  ← root app (redundant)

prod/argocd/
├── application.yaml       ← direct app
└── root-application.yaml  ← root app (redundant)
```

**After (consistent):**
```
dev/argocd/
└── application.yaml

qa/argocd/
└── application.yaml

prod/argocd/
└── application.yaml
```

**Deployment Note:** When deploying these changes, you'll need to:
1. Delete the existing root applications from ArgoCD (`crewai-root-dev`, `crewai-root-qa`, `crewai-root-prod`)
2. Apply the direct applications (`crewai-dev`, `crewai-qa`, `crewai-prod`)

Would you like me to commit all the changes from this simplification work?

> AGENT

Yes, `kind: Application` is correct. This is the standard ArgoCD Application custom resource.

What we eliminated was the **app-of-apps pattern** - where a "root" Application deployed another "child" Application. Now each environment has a single direct Application that deploys the CrewAI Helm chart.

**Before (app-of-apps):**
```
root-application.yaml (kind: Application)  →  deploys  →  apps/application.yaml (kind: Application)  →  deploys  →  Helm chart
```

**After (direct):**
```
application.yaml (kind: Application)  →  deploys  →  Helm chart
```

The `kind: Application` is still needed - it's the ArgoCD CRD that tells ArgoCD what to deploy. We just removed the unnecessary intermediate layer.

> DEVELOPER

i thought is was an applicationset?

> AGENT

You're right that **ApplicationSet** would be a better fit here since you have the same application across 3 environments. An ApplicationSet uses a generator to create multiple Applications from a single template.

**Current approach (3 separate Applications):**
```
environments/dev/argocd/application.yaml   → crewai-dev
environments/qa/argocd/application.yaml    → crewai-qa
environments/prod/argocd/application.yaml  → crewai-prod
```

**ApplicationSet approach (1 template generates 3 Applications):**
```
argocd/applicationset.yaml  → generates crewai-dev, crewai-qa, crewai-prod
```

The key consideration is that your prod environment intentionally has **no automated sync** (manual deployment for safety), while dev/qa have automated sync. This can still be handled in an ApplicationSet using conditional logic.

Would you like me to convert to an ApplicationSet? I can create a single `argocd/applicationset.yaml` that:
- Uses a list generator for `[dev, qa, prod]`
- Templates the namespace, values path, and chart version
- Conditionally enables/disables automated sync per environment

> DEVELOPER

no, leave it as is

> AGENT

Understood. Keeping individual Application files per environment - they're more explicit and easier to manage per-environment differences.

- Eliminated app-of-apps pattern (deleted root-application.yaml files)
- Added missing ignoreDifferences to QA and prod
- Consistent structure across all environments

    14→  finalizers:
    15→    - resources-finalizer.argocd.argoproj.io
    16→spec:
    17→  # Project assignment
    18→  project: platform
    19→
    20→  sources:
    21→    # Source 1: CrewAI Helm chart from OCI registry
    22→    - repoURL: registry.crewai.com/crewai/stable
    23→      chart: crewai-platform
    24→      targetRevision: 0.3.13
    25→      helm:
    26→        releaseName: crewai-platform
    27→        valueFiles:
    28→          - $values/environments/dev/helm/crewai/values.yaml
    29→
    30→    # Source 2: Git repo for Helm values (referenced by $values)
    31→    - repoURL: https://github.com/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai
    32→      targetRevision: main
    33→      ref: values
    34→
    35→  destination:
    36→    server: https://kubernetes.default.svc
    37→    namespace: crewai
    38→
    39→  syncPolicy:
    40→    automated:
    41→      # Automatically sync when Git changes are detected
    42→      prune: true
    43→      # Automatically correct drift
    44→      selfHeal: true
    45→      # Allow empty resources
    46→      allowEmpty: false
    47→    syncOptions:
    48→      # Create namespace if it doesn't exist
    49→      - CreateNamespace=true
    50→      # Validate resources before applying
    51→      - Validate=true
    52→      # Use server-side apply
    53→      - ServerSideApply=true
    54→      # Respect ignore differences
    55→      - RespectIgnoreDifferences=true
    56→    retry:
    57→      limit: 5
    58→      backoff:
    59→        duration: 5s
    60→        factor: 2
    61→        maxDuration: 3m
    62→
    63→  ignoreDifferences:
    64→    # Ignore differences in replicas (managed by HPA)
    65→    - group: apps
    66→      kind: Deployment
    67→      jsonPointers:
    68→        - /spec/replicas
    69→    # Ignore resource adjustments by GKE Autopilot
    70→    - group: apps
    71→      kind: Deployment
    72→      jqPathExpressions:
    73→        - .spec.template.spec.containers[].resources
    74→    # Ignore differences in annotations added by GKE
    75→    - group: ""
    76→      kind: Service
    77→      jsonPointers:
    78→        - /metadata/annotations/cloud.google.com~1neg
    79→        - /metadata/annotations/cloud.google.com~1neg-status
    80→    # Ignore Secret data changes (managed by Terraform)
    81→    - group: ""
    82→      kind: Secret
    83→      jsonPointers:
    84→        - /data
    85→    # Ignore HTTPRoute field defaults added by Server-Side Apply
    86→    - group: gateway.networking.k8s.io
    87→      kind: HTTPRoute
    88→      jqPathExpressions:
    89→        - .spec.parentRefs[].group
    90→        - .spec.parentRefs[].kind
    91→        - .spec.rules[].backendRefs[].group
    92→        - .spec.rules[].backendRefs[].kind
    93→        - .spec.rules[].backendRefs[].weight
    94→
    95→  # Health assessment
    96→  revisionHistoryLimit: 10
    97→

```
name: Deploy ArgoCD Application

on:
  workflow_dispatch:
    inputs:
      environment:
        description: 'Target environment'
        required: true
        type: choice
        options:
          - dev
          - qa
          - prod

concurrency:
  group: argocd-deploy-${{ inputs.environment }}
  cancel-in-progress: false

permissions:
  contents: read

jobs:
  check-branch:
    name: Check Branch and Environment
    runs-on: static-ip-runner
    steps:
      - name: Validate branch-environment combination
        run: |
          BRANCH="${{ github.ref_name }}"
          ENV="${{ inputs.environment }}"

          if [[ "$BRANCH" =~ ^v[0-9]+\.[0-9]+\.[0-9]+$ ]]; then
            BRANCH_TYPE="tag"
          elif [[ "$BRANCH" == "main" ]]; then
            BRANCH_TYPE="main"
          elif [[ "$BRANCH" == "develop" ]]; then
            BRANCH_TYPE="develop"
          else
            echo "::error::Deployments not allowed from branch: $BRANCH"
            echo "::error::Allowed branches: main, develop, v* tags"
            exit 1
          fi

          case "$BRANCH_TYPE" in
            tag)
              if [[ "$ENV" != "prod" ]]; then
                echo "::error::Version tags (v*) can only deploy to production"
                echo "::error::Current: $BRANCH -> $ENV"
                exit 1
              fi
              ;;
            main)
              if [[ "$ENV" != "dev" && "$ENV" != "qa" ]]; then
                echo "::error::Main branch can deploy to: dev, qa (not $ENV)"
                echo "::error::For production, create a version tag"
                exit 1
              fi
              ;;
            develop)
              if [[ "$ENV" != "dev" ]]; then
                echo "::error::Develop branch can only deploy to dev environment"
                echo "::error::Current: $BRANCH -> $ENV"
                exit 1
              fi
              ;;
          esac

          echo "Branch-environment check passed: $BRANCH -> $ENV"
          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "## Access Control" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "- **Branch:** $BRANCH ($BRANCH_TYPE)" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "- **Environment:** $ENV" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "- **Status:** Authorized" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY

  deploy:
    name: Deploy to ${{ inputs.environment }}
    needs: check-branch
    uses: konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/argocd-deploy.yml@main
    with:
      application_file: environments/${{ inputs.environment }}/argocd/application.yaml
    secrets:
      argocd_auth_token: ${{ secrets.ARGOCD_AUTH_TOKEN }}

  summary:
    name: Deployment Summary
    needs: [check-branch, deploy]
    if: always()
    runs-on: static-ip-runner
    steps:
      - name: Generate Summary
        run: |
          echo "## ArgoCD Deployment Pipeline" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY

          OVERALL_STATUS="Deployed"
          if [ "${{ needs.deploy.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
            OVERALL_STATUS="Failed"
          elif [ "${{ needs.check-branch.result }}" == "failure" ]; then
            OVERALL_STATUS="Unauthorized"
          elif [ "${{ needs.deploy.result }}" == "skipped" ]; then
            OVERALL_STATUS="Skipped"
          fi

          echo "### Overall Status: $OVERALL_STATUS" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "**Environment:** ${{ inputs.environment }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "**Application:** \`${{ needs.deploy.outputs.app_name || 'N/A' }}\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "**Sync Status:** ${{ needs.deploy.outputs.sync_status || 'N/A' }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "**Branch:** ${{ github.ref_name }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "**Triggered by:** @${{ github.actor }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY

          echo "**Job Results:**" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "- Branch Check: ${{ needs.check-branch.result }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "- Deploy: ${{ needs.deploy.result }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY

      - name: Check Overall Status
        if: needs.deploy.result == 'failure'
        run: |
          echo "::error::ArgoCD deployment failed. Check deploy job logs."
          exit 1
```

  "content": "name: Deploy ArgoCD Application\n\non:\n  workflow_dispatch:\n    inputs:\n      environment:\n        description: 'Target environment'\n        required: true\n        type: choice\n        options:\n          - dev\n          - qa\n          - prod\n\nconcurrency:\n  group: argocd-deploy-${{ inputs.environment }}\n  cancel-in-progress: false\n\npermissions:\n  contents: read\n\njobs:\n  check-branch:\n    name: Check Branch and Environment\n    runs-on: static-ip-runner\n    steps:\n      - name: Validate branch-environment combination\n        run: |\n          BRANCH=\"${{ github.ref_name }}\"\n          ENV=\"${{ inputs.environment }}\"\n\n          if [[ \"$BRANCH\" =~ ^v[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+$ ]]; then\n            BRANCH_TYPE=\"tag\"\n          elif [[ \"$BRANCH\" == \"main\" ]]; then\n            BRANCH_TYPE=\"main\"\n          elif [[ \"$BRANCH\" == \"develop\" ]]; then\n            BRANCH_TYPE=\"develop\"\n          else\n            echo \"::error::Deployments not allowed from branch: $BRANCH\"\n            echo \"::error::Allowed branches: main, develop, v* tags\"\n            exit 1\n          fi\n\n          case \"$BRANCH_TYPE\" in\n            tag)\n              if [[ \"$ENV\" != \"prod\" ]]; then\n                echo \"::error::Version tags (v*) can only deploy to production\"\n                echo \"::error::Current: $BRANCH -> $ENV\"\n                exit 1\n              fi\n              ;;\n            main)\n              if [[ \"$ENV\" != \"dev\" && \"$ENV\" != \"qa\" ]]; then\n                echo \"::error::Main branch can deploy to: dev, qa (not $ENV)\"\n                echo \"::error::For production, create a version tag\"\n                exit 1\n              fi\n              ;;\n            develop)\n              if [[ \"$ENV\" != \"dev\" ]]; then\n                echo \"::error::Develop branch can only deploy to dev environment\"\n                echo \"::error::Current: $BRANCH -> $ENV\"\n                exit 1\n              fi\n              ;;\n          esac\n\n          echo \"Branch-environment check passed: $BRANCH -> $ENV\"\n          echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"## Access Control\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"- **Branch:** $BRANCH ($BRANCH_TYPE)\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"- **Environment:** $ENV\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"- **Status:** Authorized\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n\n  deploy:\n    name: Deploy to ${{ inputs.environment }}\n    needs: check-branch\n    uses: konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/argocd-deploy.yml@main\n    with:\n      application_file: environments/${{ inputs.environment }}/argocd/application.yaml\n    secrets:\n      argocd_auth_token: ${{ secrets.ARGOCD_AUTH_TOKEN }}\n\n  summary:\n    name: Deployment Summary\n    needs: [check-branch, deploy]\n    if: always()\n    runs-on: static-ip-runner\n    steps:\n      - name: Generate Summary\n        run: |\n          echo \"## ArgoCD Deployment Pipeline\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n\n          OVERALL_STATUS=\"Deployed\"\n          if [ \"${{ needs.deploy.result }}\" == \"failure\" ]; then\n            OVERALL_STATUS=\"Failed\"\n          elif [ \"${{ needs.check-branch.result }}\" == \"failure\" ]; then\n            OVERALL_STATUS=\"Unauthorized\"\n          elif [ \"${{ needs.deploy.result }}\" == \"skipped\" ]; then\n            OVERALL_STATUS=\"Skipped\"\n          fi\n\n          echo \"### Overall Status: $OVERALL_STATUS\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"**Environment:** ${{ inputs.environment }}\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"**Application:** \\`${{ needs.deploy.outputs.app_name || 'N/A' }}\\`\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"**Sync Status:** ${{ needs.deploy.outputs.sync_status || 'N/A' }}\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"**Branch:** ${{ github.ref_name }}\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"**Triggered by:** @${{ github.actor }}\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n\n          echo \"**Job Results:**\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"- Branch Check: ${{ needs.check-branch.result }}\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"- Deploy: ${{ needs.deploy.result }}\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n\n      - name: Check Overall Status\n        if: needs.deploy.result == 'failure'\n        run: |\n          echo \"::error::ArgoCD deployment failed. Check deploy job logs.\"\n          exit 1\n"
}
```

> AGENT

Done. The workflow now uses the reusable workflow from `.github-private` - identical to Backstage.

**Key change:**
```yaml
# Before (inline logic)
deploy:
  steps:
    - name: Install ArgoCD CLI
    - name: Deploy ArgoCD Application
      run: |
        # 30+ lines of inline bash...

# After (reusable workflow)
deploy:
  uses: konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/argocd-deploy.yml@main
  with:
    application_file: environments/${{ inputs.environment }}/argocd/application.yaml
  secrets:
    argocd_auth_token: ${{ secrets.ARGOCD_AUTH_TOKEN }}
```

> AGENT

This is a repository access issue. The `.github-private` repo's reusable workflows need to be explicitly shared with calling repos.

**To fix (in GitHub):**
1. Go to `konecta-ix-services/.github-private` → Settings → Actions → General
2. Under "Access", ensure "Accessible from repositories in the organization" is selected
3. Or add `kix-services-crewai` to the allowed list

**Alternative:** If you can't change those settings, I can revert to the inline workflow that was working before. Want me to do that?

> DEVELOPER

its set to Accessible from repositories in the 'konecta-ix-services' organization
Workflows in other repositories that are part of the 'konecta-ix-services' organization can access the actions and reusable workflows in this repository. Access is allowed only from private repositories.

### Priority 6: Add Drift Detection (Low Effort, High Value)

**Solution:** GitHub Actions workflow for daily Terraform plan:

```yaml
name: Terraform Drift Detection
on:
  schedule:
    - cron: '0 6 * * *'
  workflow_dispatch:

jobs:
  detect-drift:
    strategy:
      matrix:
        environment: [dev, qa, prod]
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: hashicorp/setup-terraform@v3
      - run: |
          cd environments/${{ matrix.environment }}/terraform
          terraform init
          terraform plan -detailed-exitcode
```

---

## Implementation Roadmap

### Phase 1: Quick Wins (1-2 weeks)

```
name: z_Terraform Plan (Reusable)

# Reusable workflow for running Terraform plan in service repositories
# Simplified from platform-infrastructure: no private modules, no backend overrides
on:
  workflow_call:
    inputs:
      working_directory:
        description: 'Terraform directory to plan'
        required: true
        type: string
      terraform_version:
        description: 'Terraform version to use'
        required: false
        type: string
        default: '1.7.4'
      environment:
        description: 'Environment name (dev, qa, prod)'
        required: true
        type: string
      comment_on_pr:
        description: 'Post plan summary as PR comment'
        required: false
        type: boolean
        default: true
      upload_artifact:
        description: 'Upload plan file as artifact'
        required: false
        type: boolean
        default: false
    outputs:
      plan_status:
        description: 'Plan status (success, failure, or no_changes)'
        value: ${{ jobs.plan.outputs.status }}
      changes_detected:
        description: 'Whether changes were detected (true/false)'
        value: ${{ jobs.plan.outputs.changes_detected }}
      add_count:
        description: 'Number of resources to add'
        value: ${{ jobs.plan.outputs.add_count }}
      change_count:
        description: 'Number of resources to change'
        value: ${{ jobs.plan.outputs.change_count }}
      destroy_count:
        description: 'Number of resources to destroy'
        value: ${{ jobs.plan.outputs.destroy_count }}

permissions:
  id-token: write
  contents: read
  pull-requests: write

jobs:
  plan:
    name: Terraform Plan
    runs-on: static-ip-runner
    environment: ${{ inputs.environment }}
    outputs:
      status: ${{ steps.plan-result.outputs.status }}
      changes_detected: ${{ steps.plan-result.outputs.changes_detected }}
      add_count: ${{ steps.extract-stats.outputs.add_count }}
      change_count: ${{ steps.extract-stats.outputs.change_count }}
      destroy_count: ${{ steps.extract-stats.outputs.destroy_count }}

    steps:
      - name: Checkout code
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Setup Terraform
        uses: hashicorp/setup-terraform@v3
        with:
          terraform_version: ${{ inputs.terraform_version }}
          terraform_wrapper: false

      - name: Configure GCP Auth
        id: gcp-auth-config
        run: |
          # Read from calling repo's environment variables
          PROJECT_NUMBER="${{ vars.GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER }}"
          SERVICE_NAME="${{ vars.SERVICE_NAME }}"

          if [ -z "$PROJECT_NUMBER" ] || [ -z "$SERVICE_NAME" ]; then
            echo "::error::Missing required environment variables."
            echo "::error::Set GCP_PROJECT_NUMBER and SERVICE_NAME as GitHub Environment variables for '${{ inputs.environment }}'."
            exit 1
          fi

          ENV="${{ inputs.environment }}"
          POOL_ID="github-${SERVICE_NAME}-${ENV}"
          PROVIDER="projects/${PROJECT_NUMBER}/locations/global/workloadIdentityPools/${POOL_ID}/providers/github-provider"
          SA="${POOL_ID}@kd-ix-eur-${ENV}-gke.iam.gserviceaccount.com"

          echo "provider_id=${PROVIDER}" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
          echo "service_account=${SA}" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
          echo "Using WIF pool: ${POOL_ID} (project ${PROJECT_NUMBER})"
          echo "Service account: ${SA}"

      - name: Authenticate to GCP
        uses: google-github-actions/auth@v2
        with:
          workload_identity_provider: ${{ steps.gcp-auth-config.outputs.provider_id }}
          service_account: ${{ steps.gcp-auth-config.outputs.service_account }}

      - name: Setup Cloud SDK
        uses: google-github-actions/setup-gcloud@v2

      - name: Terraform Init
        run: terraform init
        working-directory: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}

      - name: Terraform Plan
        id: plan
        run: |
          terraform plan -out=tfplan -no-color 2>&1 | tee plan_output.txt
          EXIT_CODE=${PIPESTATUS[0]}
          echo "exit_code=$EXIT_CODE" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
          exit $EXIT_CODE
        working-directory: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}
        continue-on-error: true

      - name: Terraform Show
        id: show
        if: steps.plan.outcome == 'success'
        run: |
          terraform show -no-color tfplan > plan_readable.txt
          head -n 50 plan_readable.txt > plan_summary.txt
        working-directory: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}

      - name: Check for changes
        id: check-changes
        if: steps.plan.outcome == 'success'
        run: |
          if grep -q "No changes" plan_readable.txt; then
            echo "changes_detected=false" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
          else
            echo "changes_detected=true" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
          fi
        working-directory: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}

      - name: Extract plan statistics
        id: extract-stats
        if: steps.plan.outcome == 'success'
        run: |
          ADD_COUNT=$(grep -oP 'Plan: \K\d+(?= to add)' plan_output.txt || echo "0")
          CHANGE_COUNT=$(grep -oP '\K\d+(?= to change)' plan_output.txt || echo "0")
          DESTROY_COUNT=$(grep -oP '\K\d+(?= to destroy)' plan_output.txt || echo "0")

          cat > plan_stats_${{ inputs.environment }}.json <<EOF
          {
            "directory": "${{ inputs.working_directory }}",
            "environment": "${{ inputs.environment }}",
            "status": "success",
            "add": ${ADD_COUNT},
            "change": ${CHANGE_COUNT},
            "destroy": ${DESTROY_COUNT}
          }
          EOF

          echo "add_count=${ADD_COUNT}" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
          echo "change_count=${CHANGE_COUNT}" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
          echo "destroy_count=${DESTROY_COUNT}" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
        working-directory: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}

      - name: Create failure statistics
        if: steps.plan.outcome == 'failure'
        run: |
          cat > plan_stats_${{ inputs.environment }}.json <<EOF
          {
            "directory": "${{ inputs.working_directory }}",
            "environment": "${{ inputs.environment }}",
            "status": "failure",
            "add": 0,
            "change": 0,
            "destroy": 0
          }
          EOF
        working-directory: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}

      - name: Upload plan statistics
        if: always()
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: plan-stats-${{ inputs.environment }}-${{ hashFiles(inputs.working_directory) }}
          path: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}/plan_stats_${{ inputs.environment }}.json
          retention-days: 7

      - name: Comment on PR
        if: inputs.comment_on_pr && github.event_name == 'pull_request' && steps.plan.outcome == 'success'
        uses: actions/github-script@v7
        with:
          script: |
            const fs = require('fs');
            const planSummary = fs.readFileSync('${{ inputs.working_directory }}/plan_summary.txt', 'utf8');
            const planOutput = fs.readFileSync('${{ inputs.working_directory }}/plan_output.txt', 'utf8');

            const addMatch = planOutput.match(/Plan: (\d+) to add/);
            const changeMatch = planOutput.match(/(\d+) to change/);
            const destroyMatch = planOutput.match(/(\d+) to destroy/);

            const toAdd = addMatch ? addMatch[1] : '0';
            const toChange = changeMatch ? changeMatch[1] : '0';
            const toDestroy = destroyMatch ? destroyMatch[1] : '0';

            const body = `## Terraform Plan: \`${{ inputs.working_directory }}\`

            **Environment:** ${{ inputs.environment }}
            **Changes:** ${toAdd} to add, ${toChange} to change, ${toDestroy} to destroy

            <details>
            <summary>Show Plan</summary>

            \`\`\`terraform
            ${planSummary}
            \`\`\`

            </details>

            *Plan generated at: ${new Date().toISOString()}*
            `;

            github.rest.issues.createComment({
              issue_number: context.issue.number,
              owner: context.repo.owner,
              repo: context.repo.repo,
              body: body
            });

      - name: Upload plan artifact
        id: upload-plan
        if: inputs.upload_artifact && steps.plan.outcome == 'success'
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: tfplan-${{ inputs.environment }}-${{ hashFiles(inputs.working_directory) }}
          path: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}/tfplan
          retention-days: 30

      - name: Plan Result
        id: plan-result
        if: always()
        run: |
          if [ "${{ steps.plan.outcome }}" == "failure" ]; then
            echo "status=failure" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
            echo "changes_detected=false" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
            exit 1
          elif [ "${{ steps.check-changes.outputs.changes_detected }}" == "false" ]; then
            echo "status=no_changes" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
            echo "changes_detected=false" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
          else
            echo "status=success" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
            echo "changes_detected=true" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
          fi

      - name: Summary
        if: always()
        run: |
          echo "## [${{ inputs.environment }}] Terraform Plan" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "**Directory:** \`${{ inputs.working_directory }}\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "**Environment:** ${{ inputs.environment }}" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY

          if [ "${{ steps.plan.outcome }}" == "success" ]; then
            if [ "${{ steps.check-changes.outputs.changes_detected }}" == "true" ]; then
              echo "**Plan Status:** Changes detected" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
              echo "- **Changes:** ${{ steps.extract-stats.outputs.add_count }} to add, ${{ steps.extract-stats.outputs.change_count }} to change, ${{ steps.extract-stats.outputs.destroy_count }} to destroy" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
              echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
              echo "<details>" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
              echo "<summary>Plan Output (first 50 lines)</summary>" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
              echo "" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
              echo "\`\`\`terraform" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
              sed '' plan_summary.txt >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY || cat plan_summary.txt >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
              echo "\`\`\`" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
              echo "</details>" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
            else
              echo "**Plan Status:** No changes" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
            fi
          else
            echo "**Plan Status:** Failed" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY
          fi
        working-directory: ${{ inputs.working_directory }}
```

  "content": "name: Terraform Drift Detection\n\non:\n  schedule:\n    # Run daily at 6 AM UTC\n    - cron: '0 6 * * *'\n  workflow_dispatch:\n    inputs:\n      environment:\n        description: 'Environment to check (leave empty for all)'\n        required: false\n        type: choice\n        options:\n          - ''\n          - dev\n          - qa\n          - prod\n\npermissions:\n  id-token: write\n  contents: read\n  issues: write\n\njobs:\n  detect-drift-dev:\n    name: Detect Drift (dev)\n    if: github.event_name == 'schedule' || github.event.inputs.environment == '' || github.event.inputs.environment == 'dev'\n    uses: konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/terraform-plan.yml@main\n    with:\n      working_directory: environments/dev/terraform\n      environment: dev\n      comment_on_pr: false\n\n  detect-drift-qa:\n    name: Detect Drift (qa)\n    if: github.event_name == 'schedule' || github.event.inputs.environment == '' || github.event.inputs.environment == 'qa'\n    uses: konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/terraform-plan.yml@main\n    with:\n      working_directory: environments/qa/terraform\n      environment: qa\n      comment_on_pr: false\n\n  detect-drift-prod:\n    name: Detect Drift (prod)\n    if: github.event_name == 'schedule' || github.event.inputs.environment == '' || github.event.inputs.environment == 'prod'\n    uses: konecta-ix-services/.github-private/.github/workflows/terraform-plan.yml@main\n    with:\n      working_directory: environments/prod/terraform\n      environment: prod\n      comment_on_pr: false\n\n  summary:\n    name: Drift Summary\n    needs: [detect-drift-dev, detect-drift-qa, detect-drift-prod]\n    if: always()\n    runs-on: static-ip-runner\n    steps:\n      - name: Generate Summary\n        run: |\n          echo \"## Terraform Drift Detection Report\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"**Run Date:** $(date -u '+%Y-%m-%d %H:%M UTC')\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"| Environment | Status | Changes |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          echo \"|-------------|--------|---------|\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n\n          # Dev\n          DEV_STATUS=\"${{ needs.detect-drift-dev.outputs.plan_status || 'skipped' }}\"\n          DEV_CHANGES=\"${{ needs.detect-drift-dev.outputs.changes_detected || 'N/A' }}\"\n          if [ \"$DEV_STATUS\" == \"no_changes\" ]; then\n            echo \"| dev | :white_check_mark: No drift | - |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          elif [ \"$DEV_STATUS\" == \"success\" ]; then\n            echo \"| dev | :warning: Drift detected | +${{ needs.detect-drift-dev.outputs.add_count }} ~${{ needs.detect-drift-dev.outputs.change_count }} -${{ needs.detect-drift-dev.outputs.destroy_count }} |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          elif [ \"$DEV_STATUS\" == \"skipped\" ]; then\n            echo \"| dev | :fast_forward: Skipped | - |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          else\n            echo \"| dev | :x: Failed | - |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          fi\n\n          # QA\n          QA_STATUS=\"${{ needs.detect-drift-qa.outputs.plan_status || 'skipped' }}\"\n          if [ \"$QA_STATUS\" == \"no_changes\" ]; then\n            echo \"| qa | :white_check_mark: No drift | - |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          elif [ \"$QA_STATUS\" == \"success\" ]; then\n            echo \"| qa | :warning: Drift detected | +${{ needs.detect-drift-qa.outputs.add_count }} ~${{ needs.detect-drift-qa.outputs.change_count }} -${{ needs.detect-drift-qa.outputs.destroy_count }} |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          elif [ \"$QA_STATUS\" == \"skipped\" ]; then\n            echo \"| qa | :fast_forward: Skipped | - |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          else\n            echo \"| qa | :x: Failed | - |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          fi\n\n          # Prod\n          PROD_STATUS=\"${{ needs.detect-drift-prod.outputs.plan_status || 'skipped' }}\"\n          if [ \"$PROD_STATUS\" == \"no_changes\" ]; then\n            echo \"| prod | :white_check_mark: No drift | - |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          elif [ \"$PROD_STATUS\" == \"success\" ]; then\n            echo \"| prod | :warning: Drift detected | +${{ needs.detect-drift-prod.outputs.add_count }} ~${{ needs.detect-drift-prod.outputs.change_count }} -${{ needs.detect-drift-prod.outputs.destroy_count }} |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          elif [ \"$PROD_STATUS\" == \"skipped\" ]; then\n            echo \"| prod | :fast_forward: Skipped | - |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          else\n            echo \"| prod | :x: Failed | - |\" >> $GITHUB_STEP_SUMMARY\n          fi\n\n      - name: Create Issue on Drift\n        if: |\n          needs.detect-drift-dev.outputs.changes_detected == 'true' ||\n          needs.detect-drift-qa.outputs.changes_detected == 'true' ||\n          needs.detect-drift-prod.outputs.changes_detected == 'true'\n        uses: actions/github-script@v7\n        with:\n          script: |\n            const devChanges = '${{ needs.detect-drift-dev.outputs.changes_detected }}' === 'true';\n            const qaChanges = '${{ needs.detect-drift-qa.outputs.changes_detected }}' === 'true';\n            const prodChanges = '${{ needs.detect-drift-prod.outputs.changes_detected }}' === 'true';\n\n            let body = '## Terraform Drift Detected\\n\\n';\n            body += 'The nightly drift detection found configuration drift in the following environments:\\n\\n';\n\n            if (devChanges) {\n              body += `- **dev**: +${{ needs.detect-drift-dev.outputs.add_count }} ~${{ needs.detect-drift-dev.outputs.change_count }} -${{ needs.detect-drift-dev.outputs.destroy_count }}\\n`;\n            }\n            if (qaChanges) {\n              body += `- **qa**: +${{ needs.detect-drift-qa.outputs.add_count }} ~${{ needs.detect-drift-qa.outputs.change_count }} -${{ needs.detect-drift-qa.outputs.destroy_count }}\\n`;\n            }\n            if (prodChanges) {\n              body += `- **prod**: +${{ needs.detect-drift-prod.outputs.add_count }} ~${{ needs.detect-drift-prod.outputs.change_count }} -${{ needs.detect-drift-prod.outputs.destroy_count }}\\n`;\n            }\n\n            body += '\\n### Action Required\\n';\n            body += 'Review the [workflow run](' + context.serverUrl + '/' + context.repo.owner + '/' + context.repo.repo + '/actions/runs/' + context.runId + ') for details.\\n\\n';\n            body += 'Either:\\n';\n            body += '1. Apply Terraform to sync infrastructure with code\\n';\n            body += '2. Update code to match current infrastructure state\\n';\n\n            const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];\n            const title = `Terraform Drift Detected - ${today}`;\n\n            // Check for existing open drift issue\n            const existingIssues = await github.rest.issues.listForRepo({\n              owner: context.repo.owner,\n              repo: context.repo.repo,\n              state: 'open',\n              labels: 'drift-detection'\n            });\n\n            if (existingIssues.data.length === 0) {\n              await github.rest.issues.create({\n                owner: context.repo.owner,\n                repo: context.repo.repo,\n                title: title,\n                body: body,\n                labels: ['drift-detection', 'terraform']\n              });\n            } else {\n              // Update existing issue\n              await github.rest.issues.createComment({\n                owner: context.repo.owner,\n                repo: context.repo.repo,\n                issue_number: existingIssues.data[0].number,\n                body: body\n              });\n            }\n"
}

> AGENT

Done. Added nightly Terraform drift detection workflow at [.github/workflows/terraform-drift-detection.yml](.github/workflows/terraform-drift-detection.yml).

**Features:**
- Runs daily at 6 AM UTC
- Can be manually triggered for specific environments
- Uses the reusable `terraform-plan` workflow
- Creates/updates a GitHub issue when drift is detected
- Generates a summary table showing drift status per environment

     1→{{- /*
     2→Database configuration ConfigMap for Rails - PreSync bootstrap.
     3→Uses ERB templates to read values from environment variables at runtime.
     4→PreSync ensures this exists before migration job starts.
     5→
     6→This is deleted after PreSync succeeds (HookSucceeded policy), then the
     7→upstream crewai-platform chart creates the persistent ConfigMap during
     8→the main sync. This avoids duplicate resource warnings.
     9→*/ -}}
    10→{{- if .Values.databaseConfig.enabled }}
    11→apiVersion: v1
    12→kind: ConfigMap
    13→metadata:
    14→  name: {{ .Values.global.fullnameOverride }}-database-config
    15→  namespace: {{ .Release.Namespace }}
    16→  labels:
    17→    {{- include "crewai-gateway.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
    18→  annotations:
    19→    argocd.argoproj.io/hook: PreSync
    20→    argocd.argoproj.io/sync-wave: "-15"
    21→    argocd.argoproj.io/hook-delete-policy: HookSucceeded
    22→data:
    23→  database.yml: |
    24→    default: &default
    25→      adapter: postgresql
    26→      encoding: unicode

    28→      host: <%= ENV["DB_HOST"] %>
    29→      port: <%= ENV["DB_PORT"] %>
    30→      username: <%= ENV["DB_USER"] %>
    31→      # IAM authentication - no password needed (Cloud SQL Proxy handles auth)
    32→
    33→    production:
    34→      primary:
    35→        <<: *default
    36→        database: <%= ENV["POSTGRES_DB"] %>
    37→      cable:
    38→        <<: *default
    39→        database: <%= ENV["POSTGRES_CABLE_DB"] %>
    40→        migrations_paths: db/cable_migrate
    41→      oauth:
    42→        <<: *default
    43→        database: <%= ENV["POSTGRES_OAUTH_DB"] %>
    44→        migrations_paths: db/oauth_migrate
    45→{{- end }}

     1→{{- /*
     2→External Secret for postgres superuser credentials.
     3→Used by db-permissions job to grant schema access to IAM users.
     4→Runs at wave -25 (before db-permissions at -20).
     5→*/ -}}
     6→{{- if and .Values.externalSecrets.enabled .Values.databaseConfig.enabled }}
     7→apiVersion: external-secrets.io/v1beta1
     8→kind: ExternalSecret
     9→metadata:
    10→  name: {{ include "crewai-gateway.fullname" . }}-postgres
    11→  namespace: {{ .Release.Namespace }}
    12→  labels:
    13→    {{- include "crewai-gateway.labels" . | nindent 4 }}
    14→  annotations:
    15→    argocd.argoproj.io/hook: PreSync
    16→    argocd.argoproj.io/sync-wave: "-25"
    17→spec:
    18→  refreshInterval: {{ .Values.externalSecrets.refreshInterval | default "1h" }}
    19→  secretStoreRef:
    20→    name: {{ include "crewai-gateway.fullname" . }}-gcp-sm
    21→    kind: SecretStore
    22→  target:
    23→    name: {{ .Values.databasePermissions.postgresSecretName | default "postgres-credentials" }}
    24→    creationPolicy: Owner
    25→    template:
    26→      type: Opaque
    27→      metadata:
    28→        labels:
    29→          {{- include "crewai-gateway.labels" . | nindent 10 }}
    30→  data:
    31→    - secretKey: {{ .Values.databasePermissions.postgresSecretKey | default "password" }}
    32→      remoteRef:
    33→        key: {{ .Values.databasePermissions.postgresSecretGcpName | default "cloudsql-postgres-password" }}
    34→{{- end }}

> AGENT

Only dev has the local helm chart templates with `externalsecret-postgres.yaml`. QA and prod don't have this structure - they use the upstream chart with just values overrides. So the fix was only needed for dev.

The fix has been committed and pushed. ArgoCD should pick up the change and the sync should work now. The `externalSecrets.enabled: false` configuration will cause the `externalsecret-postgres.yaml` template to render nothing (since the `if` condition will be false), which is correct since ESO is managed by Terraform.

> AGENT

I see the issue now. Let me understand the full picture:

**Terraform creates:**
- ExternalSecret `crewai-credentials` → syncs to K8s secret `crewai-secrets`
- Uses platform-managed **ClusterSecretStore** `gcp-secret-manager`

**Helm chart has:**
- `externalsecret-postgres.yaml` → needs postgres superuser password for db-permissions job
- References a **SecretStore** `crewai-gcp-sm` (which was deleted)

Let me check what the db-permissions job needs.

> TOOL

{
  "file_path": "/home/nathan_booth/code/konecta/konecta-tic/kix-services-crewai/environments/dev/helm/crewai/templates/job-db-permissions.yaml"
}
