  "content": "# CLAUDE.md - CrewAI Platform Service\n\n**Repository:** kix-services-crewai\n**Purpose:** Multi-environment deployment infrastructure for CrewAI Enterprise on GKE\n\n---\n\n## Extends Master Standards\n\n> **IMPORTANT:** This file extends the **master CLAUDE.md** with service-specific guidance.\n>\n> **Universal standards (security, git workflow, operational rules) are defined in:**\n> **[kix-platform-docs/CLAUDE.md](https://github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-docs/blob/main/CLAUDE.md)**\n>\n> **This file contains:**\n> - Repository structure and architecture\n> - Terraform module composition patterns\n> - Helm values and ArgoCD configuration\n> - Environment-specific conventions\n> - Key patterns and decisions\n\n---\n\n## Repository Overview\n\nThis is the **kix-services-crewai** repository - deployment infrastructure for **CrewAI Enterprise Platform** across dev, qa, and prod environments on Google Kubernetes Engine.\n\n**Mission:** Deploy and manage CrewAI Enterprise using Terraform for infrastructure and ArgoCD for application delivery, composing remote modules from kix-platform-modules.\n\n## Repository Structure\n\n```\nkix-services-crewai/\n├── environments/\n│   ├── dev/\n│   │   ├── argocd/\n│   │   │   └── application.yaml      # ArgoCD multi-source Application (3 sources)\n│   │   ├── helm/\n│   │   │   └── crewai/\n│   │   │       ├── Chart.yaml        # Local gateway chart\n│   │   │       ├── values.yaml       # Combined values for OCI + local charts\n│   │   │       └── templates/        # Local chart templates\n│   │   │           ├── cloud-sql-proxy.yaml      # Database proxy (PreSync)\n│   │   │           ├── externalsecret-postgres.yaml  # Postgres creds (PreSync)\n│   │   │           ├── job-db-permissions.yaml   # DB permissions (PreSync)\n│   │   │           ├── configmap-database.yaml   # Rails database.yml (PreSync)\n│   │   │           ├── gateway.yaml              # Gateway API\n│   │   │           ├── httproute.yaml            # HTTP routing\n│   │   │           ├── networkpolicy.yaml        # Network policies\n│   │   │           └── ...                       # Other templates\n│   │   └── terraform/\n│   │       ├── backend.tf            # GCS state backend\n│   │       ├── database.tf           # Cloud SQL databases and IAM user\n│   │       ├── kubernetes.tf         # Namespaces, service accounts, quotas\n│   │       ├── secrets.tf            # GCP Secret Manager + ESO ExternalSecret\n│   │       ├── service-accounts.tf   # GCP service account + IAM\n│   │       └── ...\n│   ├── qa/                           # Simplified (no local chart)\n│   └── prod/                         # Simplified (no local chart)\n├── docs/\n│   ├── INDEX.md                      # Documentation index\n│   ├── SETUP.md                      # Setup instructions\n│   ├── QUICK_REFERENCE.md            # Quick reference\n│   ├── CICD.md                       # CI/CD architecture\n│   ├── runbooks/                     # Operational runbooks\n│   └── archive/                      # Historical documents\n├── CLAUDE.md                         # This file\n└── README.md                         # Main README\n```\n\n## Architecture\n\n### Deployment Model\n\n**Dev Environment (Full-Featured):**\n- **Terraform**: GCP infrastructure (databases, service accounts, IAM, buckets)\n- **Terraform**: Kubernetes resources (namespaces, service accounts, ESO ExternalSecret)\n- **ArgoCD**: OCI Helm chart (crewai-platform) + Local Helm chart (crewai-gateway)\n- **Local Chart**: Cloud SQL Proxy, Gateway API, Network Policies, PreSync hooks\n\n**QA/Prod Environments (Simplified):**\n- **Terraform**: GCP infrastructure + Kubernetes secrets\n- **ArgoCD**: OCI Helm chart only (includes sidecar Cloud SQL Proxy)\n\n### Environment Layout\n\n| Environment | GKE Project | Platform Data Project | Cloud SQL Instance |\n|------------|-------------|----------------------|-------------------|\n| dev | kd-ix-eur-dev-gke | kd-ix-eur-dev-platform-data | kd-ix-eur-dev-cloudsql-platform |\n| qa | kd-ix-eur-qa-gke | kd-ix-eur-qa-platform-data | kd-ix-eur-qa-cloudsql-platform |\n| prod | kd-ix-eur-prod-gke | kd-ix-eur-prod-platform-data | kd-ix-eur-prod-cloudsql-platform |\n\n### Namespace Pattern\n\n- `crewai` (dev) / `crewai-{env}` - Platform services (web, worker, scheduler, etc.)\n- `crewai-crews` (dev) / `crewai-crews-{env}` - Crew workload execution (isolated)\n\n## Key Patterns and Decisions\n\n### ArgoCD Multi-Source Application (Dev)\n\nDev uses **3 sources** to deploy both the upstream OCI chart and local gateway chart:\n\n```yaml\nsources:\n  # Source 1: CrewAI Helm chart from OCI registry\n  - repoURL: registry.crewai.com/crewai/stable\n    chart: crewai-platform\n    targetRevision: 0.3.13\n    helm:\n      releaseName: crewai-platform\n      valueFiles:\n        - $values/environments/dev/helm/crewai/values.yaml\n\n  # Source 2: Local gateway chart (cloud-sql-proxy, network policies, etc.)\n  - repoURL: https://github.com/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai\n    targetRevision: main\n    path: environments/dev/helm/crewai\n    helm:\n      releaseName: crewai-gateway\n      valueFiles:\n        - values.yaml\n\n  # Source 3: Git repo for Helm values (referenced by $values)\n  - repoURL: https://github.com/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-crewai\n    targetRevision: main\n    ref: values\n```\n\n### PreSync Hook Order (Dev)\n\nThe local chart uses ArgoCD sync waves for ordered initialization:\n\n| Wave | Resource | Purpose |\n|------|----------|---------|\n| -30 | cloud-sql-proxy | Deployment + Service (BeforeHookCreation policy) |\n| -25 | externalsecret-postgres | Fetch postgres superuser password via ESO |\n| -20 | job-db-permissions | Grant schema permissions to IAM user |\n| -15 | configmap-database | Rails database.yml for migrations |\n| 0 | Main application | Web, worker, buildkit, etc. |\n\n**Delete Policies:**\n- `BeforeHookCreation`: Resource persists, deleted only when replaced\n- `HookSucceeded`: Resource deleted after PreSync completes (avoids conflicts)\n\n### Cloud SQL Proxy\n\n**Dev (Standalone Service via Helm):**\n```yaml\ncloudSqlProxy:\n  enabled: true\n  connectionName: \"kd-ix-eur-dev-platform-data:europe-west1:kd-ix-eur-dev-cloudsql-platform\"\n```\n- PreSync hook at wave -30\n- IAM authentication via Workload Identity (`--auto-iam-authn`)\n- Shared service: all pods connect to `cloud-sql-proxy:5432`\n\n**QA/Prod (Sidecar via upstream chart):**\n- Extra container in each pod\n- Password authentication\n- Pods connect to `localhost:5432`\n\n### External Secrets Operator (ESO) Pattern\n\n**Dev uses Terraform + ESO** for secret management:\n\n1. **Terraform creates GCP Secret Manager secrets:**\n   - `crewai-rails-secrets-dev` (SECRET_KEY_BASE)\n   - `crewai-gcs-credentials-dev` (HMAC keys)\n   - `crewai-workos-credentials-dev` (OAuth)\n\n2. **Terraform creates ExternalSecret manifest:**\n   ```hcl\n   resource \"kubernetes_manifest\" \"external_secret_crewai_credentials\" {\n     spec = {\n       secretStoreRef = {\n         kind = \"ClusterSecretStore\"\n         name = \"gcp-secret-manager\"  # Platform-managed\n       }\n       target = {\n         name = \"crewai-secrets\"\n       }\n     }\n   }\n   ```\n\n3. **ESO syncs to K8s secret** `crewai-secrets`\n\n4. **Pods consume via envFrom:**\n   ```yaml\n   envFrom:\n     - secretRef:\n         name: \"crewai-secrets\"\n   ```\n\n**QA/Prod:** Terraform creates K8s secrets directly (no ESO).\n\n### Terraform Responsibilities\n\n**GCP Resources:**\n- Cloud SQL databases (production, cable, oauth)\n- IAM user for Cloud SQL (uses Workload Identity)\n- GCS buckets (data + logs)\n- Service account + IAM bindings\n- HMAC keys for S3-compatible GCS access\n- GCP Secret Manager secrets\n\n**Kubernetes Resources:**\n- Namespaces (crewai, crewai-crews)\n- Service accounts with Workload Identity annotation\n- Resource quotas and limit ranges\n- ExternalSecret manifest (dev only)\n\n### Helm Chart Responsibilities\n\n**Upstream OCI Chart (crewai-platform):**\n- Web, Worker, Buildkit deployments\n- Services and HPAs\n- Application configuration via values.yaml\n\n**Local Gateway Chart (crewai-gateway, dev only):**\n- Cloud SQL Proxy (PreSync)\n- Database permissions job (PreSync)\n- Database config ConfigMap (PreSync)\n- ExternalSecret for postgres password (PreSync)\n- Gateway API resources (Gateway, HTTPRoute)\n- Network policies\n- GCP Backend policies\n\n### Service Account Management\n\nTerraform creates the K8s service account:\n```hcl\nresource \"kubernetes_service_account\" \"crewai_platform\" {\n  metadata {\n    name = \"crewai-sa\"\n    annotations = {\n      \"iam.gke.io/gcp-service-account\" = google_service_account.crewai_platform.email\n    }\n  }\n}\n```\n\nHelm values reference it (no creation):\n```yaml\nserviceAccount: \"crewai-sa\"\n```\n\n## Terraform State\n\n| Environment | Backend Bucket | Prefix |\n|------------|---------------|--------|\n| dev | kd-tfstate-dev | services/crewai/resources |\n| qa | kd-tfstate-qa | services/crewai/resources |\n| prod | kd-tfstate-prod | services/crewai/resources |\n\n## Common Tasks\n\n### Deploy to Dev\n\n1. Apply Terraform (creates namespace, SA, secrets, ESO):\n   ```bash\n   cd environments/dev/terraform\n   terraform apply\n   ```\n\n2. ArgoCD syncs automatically (or trigger manually)\n\n### Upgrading CrewAI Version\n\n1. Update `targetRevision` in `environments/{env}/argocd/application.yaml`\n2. Commit and push to main\n3. ArgoCD auto-syncs (dev) or manual sync (prod)\n\n### Adding a New Secret\n\n1. Create GCP SM secret in Terraform (`secrets.tf`)\n2. Add to ExternalSecret data array (dev) or K8s secret (QA/Prod)\n3. Reference in values.yaml `envFrom` or individual env var\n\n### Rotating Credentials\n\n1. Taint the random_password resource\n2. `terraform apply` updates GCP SM → ESO syncs → pods restart\n\n## Mistakes Learned\n\n### PreSync Hook Ordering\n\nPreSync hooks must create dependencies before consumers:\n- cloud-sql-proxy (-30) before db-permissions (-20)\n- externalsecret-postgres (-25) before db-permissions (-20)\n- All PreSync before main sync (wave 0)\n\n### Hook Delete Policies\n\n- Use `BeforeHookCreation` for persistent resources (cloud-sql-proxy)\n- Use `HookSucceeded` for temporary resources that conflict with main sync\n\n### ESO ClusterSecretStore\n\nAlways use the platform-managed `ClusterSecretStore` named `gcp-secret-manager`, not namespace-scoped SecretStores. This aligns with Backstage and other services.\n\n### Environment Parity\n\nDev has additional infrastructure (local chart, Gateway API, IAM auth) that QA/Prod don't have. Test critical paths in QA before prod.\n\n## Repository Relationships\n\n- **[kix-platform-modules](https://github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules)** - Terraform modules\n- **[kix-platform-infrastructure](https://github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-infrastructure)** - Platform infrastructure\n- **[kix-services-backstage](https://github.com/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-backstage)** - Reference implementation for ESO pattern\n- **[kix-platform-docs](https://github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-docs)** - Master CLAUDE.md\n\n---\n\n**Document Version:** 2.0\n**Last Updated:** February 2026\n**Maintained By:** Platform Engineering Team\n"
}
```

   251→| Environment | Backend Bucket | Prefix |
   252→|------------|---------------|--------|
   253→| dev | kd-tfstate-dev | services/crewai/resources |
   254→| qa | kd-tfstate-qa | services/crewai/resources |
   255→| prod | kd-tfstate-prod | services/crewai/resources |
   256→
   257→## Common Tasks
   258→
   259→### Deploy to Dev
   260→
   261→1. Apply Terraform (creates namespace, SA, secrets, ESO):
   262→   ```bash
   263→   cd environments/dev/terraform
   264→   terraform apply
   265→   ```
   266→
   267→2. ArgoCD syncs automatically (or trigger manually)
   268→
   269→### Upgrading CrewAI Version
   270→
   271→1. Update `targetRevision` in `environments/{env}/argocd/application.yaml`
   272→2. Commit and push to main
   273→3. ArgoCD auto-syncs (dev) or manual sync (prod)
   274→
   275→### Adding a New Secret
   276→
   277→1. Create GCP SM secret in Terraform (`secrets.tf`)
   278→2. Add to ExternalSecret data array (dev) or K8s secret (QA/Prod)
   279→3. Reference in values.yaml `envFrom` or individual env var
   280→
   281→### Rotating Credentials
   282→
   283→1. Taint the random_password resource
   284→2. `terraform apply` updates GCP SM → ESO syncs → pods restart
   285→
   286→## Mistakes Learned
   287→
   288→### PreSync Hook Ordering
   289→
   290→PreSync hooks must create dependencies before consumers:
   291→- cloud-sql-proxy (-30) before db-permissions (-20)
   292→- externalsecret-postgres (-25) before db-permissions (-20)
   293→- All PreSync before main sync (wave 0)
   294→
   295→### Hook Delete Policies
   296→
   297→- Use `BeforeHookCreation` for persistent resources (cloud-sql-proxy)
   298→- Use `HookSucceeded` for temporary resources that conflict with main sync
   299→
   300→### ESO ClusterSecretStore
   301→
   302→Always use the platform-managed `ClusterSecretStore` named `gcp-secret-manager`, not namespace-scoped SecretStores. This aligns with Backstage and other services.
   303→
   304→### Environment Parity
   305→
   306→Dev has additional infrastructure (local chart, Gateway API, IAM auth) that QA/Prod don't have. Test critical paths in QA before prod.
   307→
   308→## Repository Relationships
   309→
   310→- **[kix-platform-modules](https://github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules)** - Terraform modules
   311→- **[kix-platform-infrastructure](https://github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-infrastructure)** - Platform infrastructure
   312→- **[kix-services-backstage](https://github.com/konecta-ix-services/kix-services-backstage)** - Reference implementation for ESO pattern
   313→- **[kix-platform-docs](https://github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-docs)** - Master CLAUDE.md
   314→
   315→---
   316→
   317→**Document Version:** 2.0
   318→**Last Updated:** February 2026
   319→**Maintained By:** Platform Engineering Team
   320→
```

     1→# CrewAI Platform - GCP Deployment
     2→
     3→Multi-environment deployment infrastructure for CrewAI Platform on Google Kubernetes Engine (GKE). 
     4→
     5→## Overview
     6→
     7→This repository contains Infrastructure as Code (Terraform) and GitOps (ArgoCD) configurations for deploying CrewAI Platform across three environments:
     8→
     9→- **Development**: `crewai.dev.ix.konecta-digital.com` (kd-ix-eur-dev-gke)
    10→- **QA**: `crewai.qa.ix.konecta-digital.com` (kd-ix-eur-qa-gke)
    11→- **Production**: `crewai.ix.konecta-digital.com` (kd-ix-eur-prod-gke)
    12→
    13→## Repository Structure
    14→
    15→```
    16→kix-services-crewai/
    17→├── environments/
    18→│   ├── dev/
    19→│   │   ├── argocd/
    20→│   │   │   └── application.yaml      # ArgoCD Application for dev
    21→│   │   ├── helm/
    22→│   │   │   └── values.yaml           # Helm values for OCI chart
    23→│   │   └── terraform/
    24→│   │       ├── backend.tf            # GCS state backend (kd-tfstate-dev)
    25→│   │       ├── main.tf               # Infrastructure + Secrets + IAM
    26→│   │       ├── outputs.tf
    27→│   │       ├── variables.tf
    28→│   │       ├── versions.tf
    29→│   │       └── README.md             # Auto-generated terraform-docs
    30→│   ├── qa/
    31→│   │   ├── argocd/application.yaml
    32→│   │   ├── helm/values.yaml
    33→│   │   └── terraform/               # Same structure as dev
    34→│   └── prod/
    35→│       ├── argocd/application.yaml
    36→│       ├── helm/values.yaml
    37→│       └── terraform/               # Same structure as dev
    38→│
    39→└── docs/
    40→    ├── prps/                         # Product Requirements & Plans
    41→    └── tasks/                        # Task breakdown documents
    42→```
    43→
    44→## Architecture
    45→
    46→- **Infrastructure**: Terraform per environment composing remote modules from [kix-platform-modules](https://github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules)
    47→- **Deployment**: External OCI Helm chart (`oci://registry.crewai.com/crewai/stable`) deployed via ArgoCD
    48→- **CI/CD**: GitHub Actions for automated testing, security scanning, and progressive deployment
    49→- **Secrets**: Terraform creates secrets in GCP Secret Manager and K8s secrets for application consumption
    50→- **Database**: Cloud SQL databases on existing PostgreSQL instances with Cloud SQL Proxy sidecar
    51→- **Storage**: GCS buckets (data + logs) with S3-compatible HMAC access
    52→- **Service Accounts**: GCP service account with Workload Identity binding to K8s service account
    53→- **Namespaces**: Dual namespaces per environment (`crewai-{env}` + `crewai-crews-{env}`) with resource quotas and network policies
    54→
    55→## Prerequisites
    56→
    57→### Required Tools
    58→
    59→- Terraform >= 1.5.0
    60→- kubectl >= 1.28
    61→- gcloud CLI
    62→- helm >= 3.12
    63→- argocd CLI (optional)
    64→
    65→### GCP Resources (Pre-existing)
    66→
    67→| Environment | GKE Cluster | Cloud SQL Instance | Project |
    68→|------------|-------------|-------------------|---------|
    69→| Dev | kd-ix-eur-dev-gke | kd-ix-eur-dev-cloudsql-platform | kd-ix-eur-dev-platform-data |
    70→| QA | kd-ix-eur-qa-gke | kd-ix-eur-qa-cloudsql-platform | kd-ix-eur-qa-platform-data |
    71→| Prod | kd-ix-eur-prod-gke | kd-ix-eur-prod-cloudsql-platform | kd-ix-eur-prod-platform-data |
    72→
    73→### GitHub Secrets
    74→
    75→The following secrets must be configured in GitHub for CI/CD workflows to function. Secrets can be set at the **repository level** (applies to all environments) or as **environment-scoped secrets** (per GitHub Environment) for environment-specific credentials.
    76→
    77→**Settings → Secrets and variables → Actions**
    78→
    79→#### Repository Secrets (all environments)
    80→
    81→| Secret | Used By | Description |
    82→|--------|---------|-------------|
    83→| `GCP_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER` | terraform-plan, terraform-apply, terraform-drift | GCP Workload Identity Federation provider ID (e.g. `projects/PROJECT_NUMBER/locations/global/workloadIdentityPools/POOL/providers/PROVIDER`). Set up via the `github-actions-oidc` module in [kix-platform-infrastructure](https://github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-infrastructure). |
    84→| `GCP_SERVICE_ACCOUNT` | terraform-plan, terraform-apply, terraform-drift | GCP service account email for GitHub Actions (e.g. `github-kix-services-crewai-dev@kd-ix-eur-dev-gke.iam.gserviceaccount.com`). |
    85→| `TERRAFORM_MODULES_APP_ID` | terraform-plan, terraform-apply, terraform-drift, terraform-validate | GitHub App ID for accessing private Terraform modules in [kix-platform-modules](https://github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules). |
    86→| `TERRAFORM_MODULES_APP_PRIVATE_KEY` | terraform-plan, terraform-apply, terraform-drift, terraform-validate | GitHub App private key (PEM) for the above App. |
    87→
    88→> **Note:** If you need different GCP credentials per environment (e.g. separate workload identity pools for dev/qa/prod), configure `GCP_WORKLOAD_IDENTITY_PROVIDER` and `GCP_SERVICE_ACCOUNT` as environment-scoped secrets on the `dev`, `qa`, and `prod` GitHub Environments instead of repository-level secrets.
    89→
    90→#### Terraform Variables (sensitive inputs)
    91→
    92→These are passed to Terraform at plan/apply time via `TF_VAR_*` environment variables. They should be stored as GitHub Actions secrets and need to be added to the terraform-plan and terraform-apply workflows.
    93→
    94→| Secret | Terraform Variable | Description |
    95→|--------|--------------------|-------------|
    96→| `TF_VAR_oauth_client_id` | `oauth_client_id` | WorkOS OAuth client ID |
    97→| `TF_VAR_oauth_api_key` | `oauth_api_key` | WorkOS OAuth API key |
    98→| `TF_VAR_oauth_domain` | `oauth_domain` | WorkOS OAuth domain |
    99→| `TF_VAR_helm_registry_token` | `helm_registry_token` | CrewAI Helm OCI registry (`registry.crewai.com`) authentication token |
   100→
   101→> **Note:** These are currently **not wired** into the GitHub Actions workflows. They must be added as `env:` blocks on the Terraform Init/Plan/Apply steps, or set as environment-scoped secrets with `TF_VAR_` prefix so Terraform picks them up automatically.
   102→
   103→#### GitHub Environments (for approval gates)
   104→
   105→The `validate-plan-apply` workflow uses GitHub Environment protection rules for deployment approvals. Create these environments in **Settings → Environments**:
   106→
   107→| Environment | Purpose |
   108→|-------------|---------|
   109→| `dev-approval` | Approval gate before applying to dev |
   110→| `qa-approval` | Approval gate before applying to QA |
   111→| `prod-approval` | Approval gate before applying to production |
   112→
   113→## CI/CD Pipeline
   114→
   115→This repository uses **GitHub Actions** for continuous integration and deployment.
   116→
   117→### Automated Workflows
   118→
   119→| Workflow | Trigger | Description |
   120→|----------|---------|-------------|
   121→| **Auto Validate and Plan** | PR to `main`/`develop` modifying `environments/*/terraform/**` | Detects changed Terraform directories, runs validation + security scan + plan |
   122→| **Validate, Plan and Apply** | Manual (`workflow_dispatch`) | Full deploy pipeline: validate → plan → approval gate → apply |
   123→| **Validate and Plan** | Manual / called by nightly | Validate + plan for a single environment (no apply) |
   124→| **Nightly Quality Checks** | Schedule (02:00 UTC daily) | Validates develop→dev, main→qa and runs drift detection on all environments |
   125→
   126→### Reusable Workflows
   127→
   128→| Workflow | Description |
   129→|----------|-------------|
   130→| `terraform-validate.yml` | Terraform fmt, validate, and Trivy security scan |
   131→| `terraform-plan.yml` | Terraform init + plan with statistics extraction |
   132→| `terraform-apply.yml` | Terraform apply with pre/post validation |
   133→| `terraform-drift.yml` | Drift detection with GitHub issue creation |
   134→
   135→### Branch-Environment Rules
   136→
   137→| Branch | Allowed Environments |
   138→|--------|---------------------|
   139→| `develop` | dev |
   140→| `main` | dev, qa |
   141→| `v*` tags | prod |
   142→
   143→### Deployment Flow
   144→
   145→```
   146→Developer → PR → Validate + Plan → Merge to develop
   147→                                            ↓
   148→                          Manual dispatch → Dev Apply (with approval)
   149→                                            ↓
   150→                          Manual dispatch → QA Apply (with approval)
   151→                                            ↓
   152→                        Tag release v* → Prod Apply (with approval, change window enforced)
   153→```
   154→
   155→## Quick Start
   156→
   157→### 1. Prerequisites Setup
   158→
   159→```bash
   160→gcloud auth login
   161→gcloud auth application-default login
   162→gcloud config set project kd-ix-eur-dev-gke
   163→gcloud container clusters get-credentials kd-ix-eur-dev-gke \
   164→  --region=europe-west1 \
   165→  --project=kd-ix-eur-dev-gke
   166→```
   167→
   168→### 2. Deploy Infrastructure (Dev)
   169→
   170→```bash
   171→cd environments/dev/terraform
   172→terraform init
   173→terraform plan
   174→terraform apply
   175→```
   176→
   177→This creates:
   178→- Cloud SQL databases (production, cable, oauth) and user on the existing instance
   179→- GCS buckets for data and logs storage
   180→- GCP service account with HMAC keys for S3-compatible GCS access
   181→- Secrets in GCP Secret Manager (database, GCS, OAuth, Helm registry)
   182→- IAM bindings (Cloud SQL client, Secret Manager accessor, GCS access, Workload Identity)
   183→- Kubernetes namespaces with resource quotas and network policies
   184→- K8s secrets for application credential consumption
   185→
   186→### 3. Deploy Application via ArgoCD
   187→
   188→Apply the ArgoCD application manifest:
   189→
   190→```bash
   191→kubectl apply -f environments/dev/argocd/application.yaml -n argocd
   192→```
   193→
   194→ArgoCD will deploy the CrewAI Platform Helm chart with environment-specific values.
   195→
   196→### 4. Verify Deployment
   197→
   198→```bash
   199→kubectl get pods -n crewai-dev
   200→kubectl get svc -n crewai-dev
   201→curl https://crewai.dev.ix.konecta-digital.com/health
   202→```
   203→
   204→## Environment Configuration
   205→
   206→### Development
   207→- 1 replica for web and worker pods
   208→- CPU: 1000m requests, 2000m limits
   209→- Memory: 2Gi requests, 4Gi limits
   210→- Auto-sync enabled in ArgoCD
   211→
   212→### QA
   213→- 2 replicas for web and worker pods
   214→- CPU: 1500m requests, 3000m limits
   215→- Memory: 4Gi requests, 8Gi limits
   216→- Auto-sync enabled in ArgoCD
   217→
   218→### Production
   219→- 3+ replicas with HPA (max 10)
   220→- CPU: 2000m requests, 4000m limits
   221→- Memory: 6Gi requests, 12Gi limits
   222→- Pod Disruption Budget: min 2 available
   223→- Pod anti-affinity and topology spread
   224→- Manual sync in ArgoCD (production safety)
   225→
   226→## Terraform
   227→
   228→Each environment's Terraform composes remote modules from [kix-platform-modules](https://github.com/konecta-ix-platform/kix-platform-modules) with inline resources:
   229→
   230→**Remote modules:**
   231→- `data-services/gcs` - GCS buckets (data + logs)
   232→- `secret-manager` - GCP Secret Manager secrets (DB, GCS, OAuth, Helm registry)
   233→- `gke-config` - Kubernetes namespaces, resource quotas, network policies, service accounts
   234→
   235→**Inline resources:**
   236→- Cloud SQL databases and user on existing instance
   237→- GCP service account (inline to avoid circular dependency with HMAC keys)
   238→- HMAC keys for S3-compatible GCS access
   239→- IAM bindings (Cloud SQL, Secret Manager, GCS, Workload Identity)
   240→- Kubernetes secrets for application credential consumption
   241→
   242→State is stored in per-environment GCS buckets (`kd-tfstate-{env}`) with prefix `services/crewai/resources`.
   243→
   244→## Maintenance
   245→
   246→### Rotating Secrets
   247→
   248→Re-run `terraform apply` in the relevant environment. Terraform manages the database password and HMAC keys - it will regenerate and update both Secret Manager and K8s secrets:
   249→
   250→```bash
   251→cd environments/dev/terraform
   252→terraform apply
   253→```
   254→
   255→### Upgrading CrewAI Version
   256→
   257→Update `targetRevision` in the environment's ArgoCD application:
   258→
   259→```bash
   260→# Edit environments/{env}/argocd/application.yaml
   261→# Change targetRevision from 0.3.10 to new version
   262→# Commit and push - ArgoCD auto-syncs (dev/qa) or manually sync (prod)
   263→```
   264→
   265→## Troubleshooting
   266→
   267→### Pods not starting
   268→
   269→```bash
   270→kubectl describe pod <pod-name> -n crewai-dev
   271→kubectl logs <pod-name> -n crewai-dev
   272→kubectl logs <pod-name> -c cloud-sql-proxy -n crewai-dev
   273→```
   274→
   275→### Secrets not available
   276→
   277→```bash
   278→kubectl get secrets -n crewai-dev
   279→cd environments/dev/terraform && terraform plan
   280→```
   281→
   282→## References
   283→
   284→- [CrewAI Enterprise Docs](https://enterprise-docs.crewai.com)
   285→- [GKE Gateway API](https://cloud.google.com/kubernetes-engine/docs/concepts/gateway-api)
   286→- [ArgoCD](https://argo-cd.readthedocs.io)
   287→
   288→## Documentation
   289→
   290→Comprehensive documentation for setup, operations, and architecture.
   291→
   292→**Getting Started:**
   293→1. [CLAUDE.md](CLAUDE.md) - Architecture patterns and key decisions
   294→2. [docs/SETUP.md](docs/SETUP.md) - Complete setup guide (5 phases)
   295→3. [docs/QUICK_REFERENCE.md](docs/QUICK_REFERENCE.md) - Common commands
   296→4. [docs/INDEX.md](docs/INDEX.md) - Full documentation index
   297→
   298→**Daily Operations:**
   299→- [docs/QUICK_REFERENCE.md](docs/QUICK_REFERENCE.md) - Commands and workflows
   300→- [docs/CICD.md](docs/CICD.md) - CI/CD pipeline details
   301→- [docs/runbooks/](docs/runbooks/) - Emergency procedures
   302→
   303→**Architecture & Design:**
   304→- [CLAUDE.md](CLAUDE.md) - Key patterns, decisions, and lessons learned
   305→- [docs/CREW_NAMESPACE.md](docs/CREW_NAMESPACE.md) - Crew namespace architecture
   306→- [docs/GATEWAY_NETWORKING.md](docs/GATEWAY_NETWORKING.md) - Gateway API and networking
   307→
   308→**Contributing to Documentation:**
   309→- [docs/DOCUMENTATION.md](docs/DOCUMENTATION.md) - How to maintain and contribute to documentation
   310→
   311→## License
   312→
   313→Internal use only - Konecta Digital Platform
   314→
